<a id="problem-639"></a>
## 639. Decode Ways II

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/decode-ways-ii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593697005/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-01 22:46 UTC

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int numDecodings(string s) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        int n = s.size();
        vector<long long> dp(n + 1, 0);
        dp[0] = 1;
        dp[1] = s[0] == '0' ? 0 : (s[0] == '*' ? 9 : 1);

        for (int i = 2; i <= n; ++i) {
            char c1 = s[i - 2], c2 = s[i - 1];

            if (c2 == '*') {
                dp[i] = (dp[i] + 9 * dp[i - 1]) % MOD;
            } else if (c2 != '0') {
                dp[i] = (dp[i] + dp[i - 1]) % MOD;
            }

            if (c1 == '1') {
                if (c2 == '*') dp[i] = (dp[i] + 9 * dp[i - 2]) % MOD;
                else dp[i] = (dp[i] + dp[i - 2]) % MOD;
            } else if (c1 == '2') {
                if (c2 == '*') dp[i] = (dp[i] + 6 * dp[i - 2]) % MOD;
                else if (c2 <= '6') dp[i] = (dp[i] + dp[i - 2]) % MOD;
            } else if (c1 == '*') {
                if (c2 == '*') {
                    dp[i] = (dp[i] + 15 * dp[i - 2]) % MOD;
                } else if (c2 <= '6') {
                    dp[i] = (dp[i] + 2 * dp[i - 2]) % MOD;
                } else {
                    dp[i] = (dp[i] + dp[i - 2]) % MOD;
                }
            }
        }

        return dp[n];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-646"></a>
## 646. Maximum Length of Pair Chain

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-length-of-pair-chain/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1373280296/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-08-30 14:31 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int dp[2002];
    int end_to_min_len[2002];
    int findLongestChain(vector<vector<int>>& pairs) {
        memset(dp, 0, sizeof(dp));
        memset(end_to_min_len, 0, sizeof(end_to_min_len));
        int pmax = -1001;
        int pmin = 1001;
        for(const auto& p: pairs){
            end_to_min_len[p[1]+1000] = end_to_min_len[p[1]+1000] == 0 ? p[1]-p[0] :
            min(end_to_min_len[p[1]+1000], p[1]-p[0]);
            pmin = min(p[0], pmin);
            pmax = max(p[1], pmax);
        }
        for(int i = pmin; i <= pmax; i++){
            dp[i+1001] = dp[i+1000];
            if(end_to_min_len[i+1000]){
                dp[i+1001] = max(dp[i+1001], dp[i+1000-end_to_min_len[i+1000]]+1);
            }
        }
        return dp[pmax+1001];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-647"></a>
## 647. Palindromic Substrings

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/palindromic-substrings/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1541360778/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-02-13 06:01 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int countSubstrings(string s) {
        int n = s.size();
        int count = 0;

        // Helper lambda function to expand around center
        auto expandAroundCenter = [&](int left, int right) {
            while (left >= 0 && right < n && s[left] == s[right]) {
                count++; // Count the palindrome
                left--;
                right++;
            }
        };

        // Iterate through each possible center
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            // Odd-length palindromes (single center)
            expandAroundCenter(i, i);

            // Even-length palindromes (two-center)
            expandAroundCenter(i, i + 1);
        }

        return count;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-650"></a>
## 650. 2 Keys Keyboard

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/2-keys-keyboard/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1416382986/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-10-08 22:57 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
    int minSteps(int n) 
	{
        int curr = 1 ;
		int steps = 0;
		int copy = 0 ;
		while(curr < n)
		{
			if ( (n-curr)%curr ==0)  
			{
				steps += 2 ;
				copy = curr ;
			}
			else
			{
				steps += 1 ;
			}
			curr += copy ;
		}
		return steps ;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-664"></a>
## 664. Strange Printer

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/strange-printer/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593853353/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-02 02:58 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <string>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int strangePrinter(string s) {
        int n = s.size();
        vector<vector<int>> dp(n, vector<int>(n, 0));
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
            dp[i][i] = 1;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                dp[i][j] = dp[i + 1][j] + 1;
                for (int k = i + 1; k <= j; ++k)
                    if (s[i] == s[k])
                        dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j], dp[i + 1][k - 1] + dp[k][j]);
            }
        }
        return dp[0][n - 1];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-673"></a>
## 673. Number of Longest Increasing Subsequence

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/number-of-longest-increasing-subsequence/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1501843838/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-01-08 13:30 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int findNumberOfLIS(vector<int>& nums) {
        if (nums.empty()) return 0;
        
        int n = nums.size();
        vector<int> dp(n, 1);  // dp[i] will store the length of LIS ending at i
        vector<int> count(n, 1);  // count[i] will store the number of LIS ending at i
        
        int maxLength = 1;
        int totalCount = 0;
        
        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] > nums[j]) {
                    if (dp[j] + 1 > dp[i]) {
                        dp[i] = dp[j] + 1;
                        count[i] = count[j];  // reset the count to count[j]
                    } else if (dp[j] + 1 == dp[i]) {
                        count[i] += count[j];  // add count[j] to count[i]
                    }
                }
            }
            maxLength = max(maxLength, dp[i]);  // update maxLength
        }
        
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            if (dp[i] == maxLength) {
                totalCount += count[i];
            }
        }
        
        return totalCount;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-678"></a>
## 678. Valid Parenthesis String

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/valid-parenthesis-string/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1501813055/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-01-08 12:54 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
    bool checkValidString(string s) 
    {
        int length = s.length() - 1;
        int openCount = 0, closedCount = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i <= length; i++)
        {
	        if (s[i] == '*' || s[i] == '(') 
                openCount++;
	        else 
                openCount--;

	        if (s[length - i] == '*' || s[length - i] == ')') 
                closedCount++;
	        else 
                closedCount--;
	
            if (openCount < 0 || closedCount < 0) 
                return false;
        }
        return true;        
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-688"></a>
## 688. Knight Probability in Chessboard

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/knight-probability-in-chessboard/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1166081683/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-02-04 18:14 UTC

```cpp
class Solution
{
public:
    double f(int n, int k, int nr, int nc, vector<vector<vector<double>>>& dp)
    {
        if (nc < 0 || nr < 0 || nc >= n || nr >= n) return 0;
        if (k == 0) return 1;
        if (dp[nr][nc][k] != -1) return dp[nr][nc][k];
        int r[] = { -2,-2,-1,1,2,2,1,-1 };
        int c[] = { -1,1,2,2,1,-1,-2,-2 };
        double prob = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
        {
            prob += f(n, k - 1, nr + r[i], nc + c[i], dp) / 8.0;
        }
        return dp[nr][nc][k] = prob;
    }

    double knightProbability(int n, int k, int row, int col) 
    {
        if (k == 0) return 1;
        vector<vector<vector<double>>> dp(n, vector<vector<double>>(n, vector<double>(k + 1, -1)));
        return f(n, k, row, col, dp);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-689"></a>
## 689. Maximum Sum of 3 Non-Overlapping Subarrays

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-sum-of-3-non-overlapping-subarrays/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1590135644/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-29 14:52 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    vector<int> maxSumOfThreeSubarrays(vector<int>& nums, int k) {
        int n = nums.size();
        vector<int> sum(n + 1, 0), left(n, 0), right(n, n - k);
        
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) sum[i + 1] = sum[i] + nums[i];

        int maxSum = sum[k] - sum[0];
        for (int i = k; i < n; i++) {
            if (sum[i + 1] - sum[i + 1 - k] > maxSum) {
                left[i] = i + 1 - k;
                maxSum = sum[i + 1] - sum[i + 1 - k];
            } else {
                left[i] = left[i - 1];
            }
        }

        maxSum = sum[n] - sum[n - k];
        for (int i = n - k - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            if (sum[i + k] - sum[i] >= maxSum) {
                right[i] = i;
                maxSum = sum[i + k] - sum[i];
            } else {
                right[i] = right[i + 1];
            }
        }

        vector<int> result;
        int maxTotal = 0;
        for (int i = k; i <= n - 2 * k; i++) {
            int l = left[i - 1], r = right[i + k];
            int total = (sum[i + k] - sum[i]) + (sum[l + k] - sum[l]) + (sum[r + k] - sum[r]);
            if (total > maxTotal) {
                maxTotal = total;
                result = {l, i, r};
            }
        }
        
        return result;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-691"></a>
## 691. Stickers to Spell Word

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/stickers-to-spell-word/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1595382409/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-03 09:01 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minStickers(vector<string>& stickers, string target) {
        int m = target.size();
        vector<vector<int>> stickerCount;
        for (string& s : stickers) {
            vector<int> count(26, 0);
            for (char c : s) count[c - 'a']++;
            stickerCount.push_back(count);
        }

        unordered_map<string, int> memo;
        memo[""] = 0;

        function<int(string)> dp = [&](string t) -> int {
            if (memo.count(t)) return memo[t];
            vector<int> count(26, 0);
            for (char c : t) count[c - 'a']++;

            int res = INT_MAX;
            for (auto& s : stickerCount) {
                if (s[t[0] - 'a'] == 0) continue;
                string next;
                for (int i = 0; i < 26; ++i) {
                    if (count[i] > s[i])
                        next += string(count[i] - s[i], 'a' + i);
                }
                int temp = dp(next);
                if (temp != -1) res = min(res, 1 + temp);
            }
            return memo[t] = res == INT_MAX ? -1 : res;
        };

        return dp(target);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-698"></a>
## 698. Partition to K Equal Sum Subsets

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/partition-to-k-equal-sum-subsets/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1536892013/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-02-09 11:05 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    bool canPartitionKSubsets(vector<int>& nums, int k) {
        int sum = accumulate(nums.begin(), nums.end(), 0);
        if (sum % k != 0) return false;
        int target = sum / k;

        sort(nums.rbegin(), nums.rend()); // Sort in descending order to optimize
        vector<int> buckets(k, 0);

        return backtrack(nums, buckets, 0, target);
    }

private:
    bool backtrack(const vector<int>& nums, vector<int>& buckets, int index, int target) {
        if (index == nums.size())
            return all_of(buckets.begin(), buckets.end(), [&](int sum) { return sum == target; });

        for (int i = 0; i < buckets.size(); ++i) {
            if (buckets[i] + nums[index] > target) continue;

            buckets[i] += nums[index];
            if (backtrack(nums, buckets, index + 1, target)) return true;
            buckets[i] -= nums[index];

            if (buckets[i] == 0) break; // Prune duplicate states
        }

        return false;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-714"></a>
## 714. Best Time to Buy and Sell Stock with Transaction Fee

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/best-time-to-buy-and-sell-stock-with-transaction-fee/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1528739455/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-02-02 14:49 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
    int maxProfit(vector<int>& p, int fee) 
    {
        int n = p.size();
        vector<vector<int>> dp(n+1, vector<int>(2, 0));

        for(int i = n-1; i>=0; i--)
        for(int hold = 0; hold<=1; hold++)
        {
                if(hold)
                    dp[i][hold] = max( p[i] + dp[i+1][0] - fee, dp[i+1][1] );
                else
                    dp[i][hold] = max( -p[i] + dp[i+1][1], dp[i+1][0] );
        }
        return dp[0][0];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-718"></a>
## 718. Maximum Length of Repeated Subarray

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-length-of-repeated-subarray/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1514648697/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-01-20 14:22 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
     int dp[1001][1001];
     int findLength(vector<int>& nums1, vector<int>& nums2) 
     {
        int ans=0;
        memset(dp, 0, sizeof(dp));
        for(int i=1; i<=nums1.size(); i++) 
        {
            for(int j=1; j<=nums2.size(); j++) 
            {
                if(nums1[i-1]==nums2[j-1]) 
                {
                    dp[i][j]= 1+dp[i-1][j-1];
                    ans= max(ans, dp[i][j]);
                }
            }
        }
        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-730"></a>
## 730. Count Different Palindromic Subsequences

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/count-different-palindromic-subsequences/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1591368684/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-30 19:04 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    
    int dp[1001][1001];
    int mod = 1000*1000*1000+7;
    int dfs(int i, int j, string &s) {
        if(i > j) return 0;
        if(i==j) return 1;
        if(dp[i][j]) return dp[i][j];
        int ans=0;
        if(s[i]==s[j]) {
            ans = 2*dfs(i+1,j-1,s);
            int l=i+1, r=j-1;
            while(l<=r and s[l]!=s[i]) l++;
            while(l<=r and s[r]!=s[j]) r--;
            if(l < r) ans-=dfs(l+1,r-1,s);
            else if(l==r) ans++;
            else ans+=2;
        }
        else ans = dfs(i+1,j,s)+dfs(i,j-1,s)-dfs(i+1,j-1,s);
        return dp[i][j] = ans < 0 ? (ans+mod) % mod : ans % mod;
    }
    
    int countPalindromicSubsequences(string s) {
        memset(dp,0,sizeof(dp));
        return dfs(0,s.size()-1,s);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-740"></a>
## 740. Delete and Earn

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/delete-and-earn/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1499152038/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-01-06 04:22 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
	unordered_map<int,int>mp;

	int deleteAndEarn(vector<int>& nums)
     {
		int n=nums.size();
		sort(nums.begin(),nums.end());
		for(auto i:nums) mp[i]++;
		vector<int> dp(n+1,0);
		for(int i=n-1;i>=0;i--)
        {
			int del=mp[nums[i]]*nums[i]+dp[upper_bound(nums.begin(),nums.end(),nums[i]+1)-nums.begin()];
			int notdel=dp[i+1];
			dp[i]=max(del,notdel);
		}
		return dp[0];
	}
};
```

<a id="problem-741"></a>
## 741. Cherry Pickup

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/cherry-pickup/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1412496910/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-10-05 11:12 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
    int solve(vector<vector<int>>& grid,int r1,int c1,int r2,int c2,int n,vector<vector<vector<int>>>& dp)
    {
        int cherries=0;
        if(r1>=n||r2>=n||c1>=n||c2>=n||grid[r1][c1]==-1||grid[r2][c2]==-1)
        {
            return INT_MIN;
        }
        if(r1==n-1&&c1==n-1) 
        {
            return grid[r1][c1];
        }
        if(dp[r1][c1][r2]!=-1) 
        {
            return dp[r1][c1][r2];
        }
       
        if(r1==r2&&c1==c2)
        {
            cherries+=grid[r1][c1];
        }
        else
        {
            cherries+=grid[r1][c1];
            cherries+=grid[r2][c2];
        }
        int f1=solve(grid,r1,c1+1,r2,c2+1,n,dp);
        int f2=solve(grid,r1+1,c1,r2,c2+1,n,dp);
        int f3=solve(grid,r1+1,c1,r2+1,c2,n,dp);
        int f4=solve(grid,r1,c1+1,r2+1,c2,n,dp);
        cherries+=max(max(f1,f2),max(f3,f4));
        return dp[r1][c1][r2]=cherries;
    }
    int cherryPickup(vector<vector<int>>& grid) 
    {
        int n=grid.size();
        int r1=0,c1=0,r2=0,c2=0;
        vector<vector<vector<int>>> dp(n+1, vector<vector<int>>(n+1, vector<int>(n+1, -1)));
        int ans=solve(grid,r1,c1,r2,c2,n,dp);
        return max(0,ans);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-746"></a>
## 746. Min Cost Climbing Stairs

**Difficulty:** Easy  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/min-cost-climbing-stairs/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1588082454/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-27 12:30 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minCostClimbingStairs(vector<int>& cost) {
        int n = cost.size();
        vector<int> dp(n + 1);
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            dp[i] = min(dp[i - 1] + cost[i - 1], dp[i - 2] + cost[i - 2]);
        }
        return dp[n];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-764"></a>
## 764. Largest Plus Sign

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/largest-plus-sign/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593693098/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-01 22:39 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <unordered_set>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int orderOfLargestPlusSign(int n, vector<vector<int>>& mines) {
        vector<vector<int>> grid(n, vector<int>(n, n));
        unordered_set<int> banned;
        for (auto& m : mines) {
            banned.insert(m[0] * n + m[1]);
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            int count = 0;
            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                count = banned.count(i * n + j) ? 0 : count + 1;
                grid[i][j] = min(grid[i][j], count);
            }
            count = 0;
            for (int j = n - 1; j >= 0; --j) {
                count = banned.count(i * n + j) ? 0 : count + 1;
                grid[i][j] = min(grid[i][j], count);
            }
        }

        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            int count = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                count = banned.count(i * n + j) ? 0 : count + 1;
                grid[i][j] = min(grid[i][j], count);
            }
            count = 0;
            for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
                count = banned.count(i * n + j) ? 0 : count + 1;
                grid[i][j] = min(grid[i][j], count);
            }
        }

        int res = 0;
        for (auto& row : grid) {
            for (int x : row)
                res = max(res, x);
        }
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-773"></a>
## 773. Sliding Puzzle

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/sliding-puzzle/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1590173923/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-29 15:21 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_set>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int slidingPuzzle(vector<vector<int>>& board) {
        string goal = "123450";
        string start = "";
        for (auto& row : board) {
            for (int num : row) {
                start += to_string(num);
            }
        }

        vector<vector<int>> neighbors = {{1, 3}, {0, 2, 4}, {1, 5}, {0, 4}, {1, 3, 5}, {2, 4}};
        queue<pair<string, int>> q;
        unordered_set<string> visited;
        q.push({start, 0});
        visited.insert(start);

        while (!q.empty()) {
            auto [curr, moves] = q.front();
            q.pop();

            if (curr == goal) return moves;

            int zeroPos = curr.find('0');
            for (int n : neighbors[zeroPos]) {
                string newState = curr;
                swap(newState[zeroPos], newState[n]);
                if (!visited.count(newState)) {
                    visited.insert(newState);
                    q.push({newState, moves + 1});
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-787"></a>
## 787. Cheapest Flights Within K Stops

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/cheapest-flights-within-k-stops/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1538659578/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-02-11 00:03 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int findCheapestPrice(int n, vector<vector<int>>& flights, int src, int dst, int k) {
    // Adjacency list to store graph: {destination, cost}
    vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> graph(n);
    for (auto& flight : flights)
        graph[flight[0]].emplace_back(flight[1], flight[2]);

    // Priority queue: {cost, current_node, stops_remaining}
    priority_queue<tuple<int, int, int>, vector<tuple<int, int, int>>, greater<>> pq;
    pq.emplace(0, src, k + 1);

    // Minimum cost to reach each node with specific stops remaining
    vector<vector<int>> minCost(n, vector<int>(k + 2, INT_MAX));
    minCost[src][k + 1] = 0;

    while (!pq.empty()) {
        auto [cost, node, stops] = pq.top();
        pq.pop();

        if (node == dst) return cost; // Destination reached with minimum cost
        if (stops > 0) {
            for (auto& [nextNode, price] : graph[node]) {
                int newCost = cost + price;
                if (newCost < minCost[nextNode][stops - 1]) {
                    minCost[nextNode][stops - 1] = newCost;
                    pq.emplace(newCost, nextNode, stops - 1);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return -1; // No valid route found
}
};
```

<a id="problem-788"></a>
## 788. Rotated Digits

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/rotated-digits/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1612877059/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-20 20:33 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int rotatedDigits(int n) {
        int count = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
            string s = to_string(i);
            bool valid = true, diff = false;
            for (char c : s) {
                if (c == '3' || c == '4' || c == '7') {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
                if (c == '2' || c == '5' || c == '6' || c == '9')
                    diff = true;
            }
            if (valid && diff) count++;
        }
        return count;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-790"></a>
## 790. Domino and Tromino Tiling

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/domino-and-tromino-tiling/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1590177677/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-29 15:24 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int numTilings(int n) {
        if (n == 1) return 1;
        if (n == 2) return 2;
        if (n == 3) return 5;
        
        vector<long long> dp(n + 1);
        dp[1] = 1; dp[2] = 2; dp[3] = 5;
        int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        
        for (int i = 4; i <= n; i++) {
            dp[i] = (2 * dp[i - 1] + dp[i - 3]) % MOD;
        }
        return dp[n];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-792"></a>
## 792. Number of Matching Subsequences

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/number-of-matching-subsequences/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1484124960/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-12-21 00:00 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
    bool check(string s1,string s2)
    {
        if(s1.length()<s2.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0, j = 0;
        while(i<s1.length() && j<s2.length())
        {
            if(s1[i]==s2[j])
            {
                i++;
                j++;
            }
            else
            {
                i++;
            }
        }
        return (j==s2.length());
    }
    int numMatchingSubseq(string s, vector<string>& words)
    {
        int ans = 0;
        unordered_map<string,int> mp;
        for(auto &i: words)
        {
            if(mp.count(i))
            {
                ans += mp[i];
            }
            else
            {
                ans += mp[i] = check(s,i);
            }
        }
        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-799"></a>
## 799. Champagne Tower

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/champagne-tower/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1592338574/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-31 18:22 UTC

```cpp

#include <vector>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    double champagneTower(int poured, int row, int col) {
        vector<vector<double>> dp(row + 1, vector<double>(row + 1, 0));
        dp[0][0] = poured;
        
        for (int i = 0; i < row; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
                double overflow = (dp[i][j] - 1) / 2;
                if (overflow > 0) {
                    dp[i + 1][j] += overflow;
                    dp[i + 1][j + 1] += overflow;
                }
            }
        }
        
        return min(1.0, dp[row][col]);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-801"></a>
## 801. Minimum Swaps To Make Sequences Increasing

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-swaps-to-make-sequences-increasing/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1369123302/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-08-26 15:51 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minSwap(vector<int>& nums1, vector<int>& nums2) {
        int ans0 = 0;
        int ans1 = 0;
        int ans00 = 0;
        for(int i = nums1.size()-1;i>=0;i--){
            for(int j = 0;j<2;j++){
                int ans = INT_MAX;
                if(j){
                    if(i==0||nums1[i]>nums2[i-1]&&nums2[i]>nums1[i-1]){
                        ans = min(ans,ans00);
                    }
                    if(i==0||nums1[i]>nums1[i-1]&&nums2[i]>nums2[i-1]){
                        ans = min(ans,1+ans1);
                    }
                }else{
                    if(i==0||nums1[i]>nums1[i-1]&&nums2[i]>nums2[i-1]){
                        ans = min(ans,ans0);
                    }
                    if(i==0||nums1[i]>nums2[i-1]&&nums2[i]>nums1[i-1]){
                        ans = min(ans,1+ans1);
                    }
                }
                if(j){
                    ans1 = ans;
                }else{
                    ans00 = ans0;
                    ans0 = ans;
                }
            }
        }
        return min(ans1,ans0);
        
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-805"></a>
## 805. Split Array With Same Average

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/split-array-with-same-average/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593853023/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-02 02:58 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_set>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    bool splitArraySameAverage(vector<int>& nums) {
        int n = nums.size(), total = accumulate(nums.begin(), nums.end(), 0);
        vector<unordered_set<int>> dp(n + 1);
        dp[0].insert(0);

        for (int a : nums)
            for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i)
                for (int s : dp[i])
                    dp[i + 1].insert(s + a);

        for (int k = 1; k < n; ++k)
            if (total * k % n == 0 && dp[k].count(total * k / n))
                return true;

        return false;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-808"></a>
## 808. Soup Servings

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/soup-servings/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610655262/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:49 UTC

```cpp
#include <unordered_map>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
    unordered_map<int, unordered_map<int, double>> memo;

    double dfs(int A, int B) {
        if (A <= 0 && B <= 0) return 0.5;
        if (A <= 0) return 1;
        if (B <= 0) return 0;
        if (memo[A][B]) return memo[A][B];

        memo[A][B] = 0.25 * (
            dfs(A - 100, B) + dfs(A - 75, B - 25) +
            dfs(A - 50, B - 50) + dfs(A - 25, B - 75)
        );
        return memo[A][B];
    }

public:
    double soupServings(int N) {
        if (N >= 5000) return 1.0;
        return dfs(N, N);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-813"></a>
## 813. Largest Sum of Averages

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/largest-sum-of-averages/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1590146599/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-29 15:01 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    double largestSumOfAverages(vector<int>& nums, int K) {
        int n = nums.size();
        vector<double> prefix(n + 1, 0);
        vector<vector<double>> dp(n + 1, vector<double>(K + 1, 0));

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            prefix[i + 1] = prefix[i] + nums[i];
            dp[i + 1][1] = prefix[i + 1] / (i + 1);
        }

        for (int k = 2; k <= K; k++) {
            for (int i = k; i <= n; i++) {
                for (int j = k - 1; j < i; j++) {
                    dp[i][k] = max(dp[i][k], dp[j][k - 1] + (prefix[i] - prefix[j]) / (i - j));
                }
            }
        }
        return dp[n][K];
    }
};
```

**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617068345/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:18 UTC

```cpp
#define ll long long
#define p pair<ll,pair<ll,ll>>
class Solution {
public:
    int racecar(int target) {
        priority_queue<p,vector<p>,greater<p>>pq;
        map<pair<ll,ll>,bool>visited;

        pq.push({0,{0,1}}); // {moves,{pos,speed}}

        while(pq.size()){
            ll moves = pq.top().first;
            ll pos = pq.top().second.first;
            ll speed = pq.top().second.second;
            pq.pop();

            if(pos == target)
                return moves;
            
            if(pos>=INT_MAX)
                continue;
            
            if(!visited[{pos,speed}]){
                visited[{pos,speed}] = 1;

                // either we can accelerate
                pq.push({moves+1,{pos+speed,speed*2}});

                // or put reverse
                // if(speed>0){
                //     pq.push({moves+1,{pos,-1}});
                // } else{
                //     pq.push({moves+1,{pos,1}});
                // }

                if ((pos + speed > target && speed > 0) || (pos + speed < target && speed < 0)) {
                     pq.push({moves+1,{pos,speed>0?-1:1}});
                }
            }
        }

        return -1;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-823"></a>
## 823. Binary Trees With Factors

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/binary-trees-with-factors/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593843051/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-02 02:45 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int numFactoredBinaryTrees(vector<int>& arr) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        sort(arr.begin(), arr.end());
        unordered_map<int, long> dp;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.size(); ++i) {
            dp[arr[i]] = 1;
            for (int j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
                if (arr[i] % arr[j] == 0 && dp.count(arr[i] / arr[j])) {
                    dp[arr[i]] = (dp[arr[i]] + dp[arr[j]] * dp[arr[i] / arr[j]]) % MOD;
                }
            }
        }

        long res = 0;
        for (auto& [k, v] : dp) res = (res + v) % MOD;
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-828"></a>
## 828. Count Unique Characters of All Substrings of a Given String

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/count-unique-characters-of-all-substrings-of-a-given-string/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617068932/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:19 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int uniqueLetterString(string s) {
        int n = s.size();
        vector<int> next(26,n);
        vector<int> nextIdx(n,n);
        vector<int> pre(26,-1);
        int ans = 0;
        for(int i = n - 1 ; i >= 0 ; i--){
            int pos = s[i] - 'A';
            if(next[pos] != n) nextIdx[i] = next[pos];   
            next[pos] = i;
        }
        for(int i = 0 ; i < n ; i++){
            int j = pre[s[i] - 'A'];
            ans += (i - j) * (nextIdx[i] - i);
            pre[s[i] - 'A'] = i;
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-834"></a>
## 834. Sum of Distances in Tree

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/sum-of-distances-in-tree/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1473586256/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-12-08 15:36 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
    vector<int> res; // to keep track distance of each node
    vector<int> count;
    void fun(int node, int parent, vector<int> adj[]) 
    {
        for (int it : adj[node]) 
        {
            if (it != parent) 
            {
                fun(it, node, adj);
                count[node] += count[it];
                res[node] += res[it] + count[it];
            }
        }
    }
        //pos-Order to calculate root dis from left and right
    void fun2(int node, int parent, vector<int> adj[],int n) 
    {
        for (int it : adj[node]) 
        {
            if (parent != it) 
            {
                res[it] = res[node] - count[it] + (n - count[it]);
                fun2(it, node, adj,n);
            }
        }
    }

    vector<int> sumOfDistancesInTree(int n, vector<vector<int>>& edges)
    {
        int sum = 0;
        int m = edges.size();
        vector<int> adj[n];
        count = vector<int>(n, 1);
        res = vector<int>(n, 0);
        for (auto& it : edges) 
        {
            int u = it[0];
            int v = it[1];
            adj[u].push_back(v);
            adj[v].push_back(u);
        }
        fun(0, -1, adj);
        fun2(0, -1, adj,n);

        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-837"></a>
## 837. New 21 Game

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/new-21-game/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593827590/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-02 02:23 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    double new21Game(int n, int k, int maxPts) {
        if (k == 0 || n >= k + maxPts) return 1.0;
        vector<double> dp(n + 1);
        dp[0] = 1.0;
        double windowSum = 1.0, res = 0.0;

        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
            dp[i] = windowSum / maxPts;
            if (i < k) windowSum += dp[i];
            else res += dp[i];
            if (i - maxPts >= 0) windowSum -= dp[i - maxPts];
        }

        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-838"></a>
## 838. Push Dominoes

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/push-dominoes/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1590134579/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-29 14:51 UTC

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    string pushDominoes(string dominoes) {
        int n = dominoes.size();
        vector<int> forces(n, 0);

        int force = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (dominoes[i] == 'R') force = n;
            else if (dominoes[i] == 'L') force = 0;
            else force = max(force - 1, 0);
            forces[i] += force;
        }

        force = 0;
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            if (dominoes[i] == 'L') force = n;
            else if (dominoes[i] == 'R') force = 0;
            else force = max(force - 1, 0);
            forces[i] -= force;
        }

        string result;
        for (int f : forces) {
            if (f > 0) result += 'R';
            else if (f < 0) result += 'L';
            else result += '.';
        }
        return result;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-847"></a>
## 847. Shortest Path Visiting All Nodes

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/shortest-path-visiting-all-nodes/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1591356770/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-30 18:49 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <unordered_set>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int shortestPathLength(vector<vector<int>>& graph) {
        int n = graph.size();
        if (n == 1) return 0;

        int allVisited = (1 << n) - 1;
        queue<pair<int, int>> q;
        vector<vector<bool>> visited(n, vector<bool>(1 << n, false));

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            q.push({i, 1 << i});
            visited[i][1 << i] = true;
        }

        int steps = 0;
        while (!q.empty()) {
            int size = q.size();
            while (size--) {
                auto [node, mask] = q.front(); q.pop();
                if (mask == allVisited) return steps;
                for (int neighbor : graph[node]) {
                    int nextMask = mask | (1 << neighbor);
                    if (!visited[neighbor][nextMask]) {
                        q.push({neighbor, nextMask});
                        visited[neighbor][nextMask] = true;
                    }
                }
            }
            steps++;
        }
        return -1;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-873"></a>
## 873. Length of Longest Fibonacci Subsequence

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/length-of-longest-fibonacci-subsequence/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617069502/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:21 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int lenLongestFibSubseq(vector<int>& arr) {
        int n = arr.size();
        vector<vector<int>> dp(n, vector<int>(n, 0));
        int maxLen = 0;

        for (int curr = 2; curr < n; curr++) {
            int start = 0, end = curr - 1;
            while (start < end) {
                int pairSum = arr[start] + arr[end];
                if (pairSum > arr[curr]) {
                    end--;
                } else if (pairSum < arr[curr]) {
                    start++;
                } else {
                    dp[end][curr] = dp[start][end] + 1;
                    maxLen = max(dp[end][curr], maxLen);
                    end--;
                    start++;
                }
            }
        }
        return maxLen == 0 ? 0 : maxLen + 2;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-877"></a>
## 877. Stone Game

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/stone-game/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1172366821/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-02-11 14:54 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    bool stoneGame(vector<int>& p) 
    {
         vector<int> dp = p;
         for (int d = 1; d < p.size(); d++)
         {
            for (int i = 0; i < p.size() - d; i++)
            {
                dp[i] = max(p[i] - dp[i + 1], p[i + d] - dp[i]);
            }
         }
        return dp[0] > 0;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-879"></a>
## 879. Profitable Schemes

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/profitable-schemes/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610608047/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 14:54 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int profitableSchemes(int n, int minProfit, vector<int>& group, vector<int>& profit) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        int m = group.size();
        vector<vector<int>> dp(n + 1, vector<int>(minProfit + 1));
        dp[0][0] = 1;
        for (int k = 0; k < m; ++k) {
            int g = group[k], p = profit[k];
            for (int i = n; i >= g; --i) {
                for (int j = minProfit; j >= 0; --j) {
                    dp[i][j] = (dp[i][j] + dp[i - g][max(0, j - p)]) % MOD;
                }
            }
        }
        int res = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i)
            res = (res + dp[i][minProfit]) % MOD;
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-887"></a>
## 887. Super Egg Drop

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/super-egg-drop/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1405888490/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-09-29 08:18 UTC

```cpp
//Memoization + Binary Search
class Solution 
{
public:
    //Time: O(n*k*logn), Space: O(n*k)
    int helper(int k, int n, vector<vector<int>>& memo)
	{
        if(n == 0 || n == 1) return n;
		
        if(k == 1) return n;
        
        if(memo[k][n] != -1) return memo[k][n];
        
        int mn = INT_MAX, low = 0, high = n, temp = 0;
        
        while(low<=high){
            
            int mid = (low + high)/2;
            
            int left = helper(k-1, mid-1, memo);
            int right = helper(k, n-mid, memo);
            
            temp = 1 + max(left, right);
            
            if(left < right) 
                low = mid+1;
            else 
                high = mid-1;     
    
            mn = min(mn, temp); 
        }
        return memo[k][n] = mn;
    }
    
    int superEggDrop(int k, int n) 
	{
        vector<vector<int>> memo(k+1, vector<int>(n+1, -1));
        return helper(k, n, memo);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-894"></a>
## 894. All Possible Full Binary Trees

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/all-possible-full-binary-trees/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1413740895/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-10-06 14:02 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
    vector<TreeNode*> allPossibleFBT(int n) 
    {
        if (n % 2 == 0) 
        {
            return {};
        }

        vector<TreeNode*> list;
        if (n == 1) 
        {
            list.push_back(new TreeNode(0));
        } 
        else 
        {
            for (int i = 1; i <= n - 1; i += 2) 
            {
                vector<TreeNode*> lTrees = allPossibleFBT(i);
                vector<TreeNode*> rTrees = allPossibleFBT(n - i - 1);

                for (TreeNode* lt : lTrees) 
                {
                    for (TreeNode* rt : rTrees) 
                    {
                        list.push_back(new TreeNode(0, lt, rt));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return list;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-898"></a>
## 898. Bitwise ORs of Subarrays

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/bitwise-ors-of-subarrays/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593827250/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-02 02:23 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <set>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int subarrayBitwiseORs(vector<int>& A) {
        unordered_set<int> res;
        set<int> cur;
        for (int a : A) {
            set<int> nxt = {a};
            for (int x : cur)
                nxt.insert(x | a);
            cur = nxt;
            res.insert(cur.begin(), cur.end());
        }
        return res.size();
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-902"></a>
## 902. Numbers At Most N Given Digit Set

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/numbers-at-most-n-given-digit-set/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593693830/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-01 22:40 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cmath>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int atMostNGivenDigitSet(vector<string>& digits, int n) {
        string strN = to_string(n);
        int len = strN.size(), d = digits.size(), res = 0;

        for (int i = 1; i < len; ++i)
            res += pow(d, i);

        for (int i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
            bool samePrefix = false;
            for (string& digit : digits) {
                if (digit[0] < strN[i]) {
                    res += pow(d, len - i - 1);
                } else if (digit[0] == strN[i]) {
                    samePrefix = true;
                }
            }
            if (!samePrefix) return res;
        }

        return res + 1;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-903"></a>
## 903. Valid Permutations for DI Sequence

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/valid-permutations-for-di-sequence/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617065901/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:10 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    #define ll long long
    const int mod = 1e9+7;
    int solve(int ind, int prev, string s, int n,vector<int>&vis,vector<vector<int>>&dp){
        if(ind>=n)return 1;
        if(dp[ind][prev]!=-1)return dp[ind][prev];

        ll ans=0;
        for(int i=0;i<=n;i++){
            if(i!=prev && vis[i]==0){
                if(s[ind]=='D' && i<prev){
                    vis[i]=1;
                    ans=(ans+solve(ind+1,i,s,n,vis,dp))%mod;
                    vis[i]=0;
                }
                if(s[ind]=='I' && i>prev){
                    vis[i]=1;
                    ans= (ans+solve(ind+1,i,s,n,vis,dp))%mod;
                    vis[i]=0;
                }
            }
        }
        return dp[ind][prev]=ans%mod;
    }
    int numPermsDISequence(string s) {
        
        int n = s.length();
        ll ans=0;
        vector<vector<int>>dp(n+1,vector<int>(n+1,-1));
        for(int i=0;i<=n;i++){
            vector<int>vis(n+2,0);
            vis[i]=1;
            
            ans= (ans+solve(0,i,s,n,vis, dp))%mod;
        }
        return ans;

    }
};
```

<a id="problem-907"></a>
## 907. Sum of Subarray Minimums

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/sum-of-subarray-minimums/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1498117691/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-01-05 04:28 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:    
    int sumSubarrayMins(vector<int>& A) 
	{ 
        int n = A.size();
        int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        vector<int> left(n), right(n);
        
        stack<int>st;
        st.push(0);
        left[0] = 1; 
        for(int i=1; i<n; i++)
        {
            while(!st.empty() && A[i] < A[st.top()]) 
                st.pop();
            
            if(st.empty()) 
                left[i] = i+1; 
            else 
                left[i] = i - st.top(); 
            
            st.push(i);
            
        }
        
        while(st.size()) 
            st.pop();
         
        st.push(n-1);
        right[n-1] = 1; 
        for(int i=n-2; i>=0; i--)
        {
            while(!st.empty() && A[i] <= A[st.top()]) 
                st.pop();
            
            if(st.empty()) 
                right[i] = n-i; // distance
            else 
                right[i] = st.top()-i;
            
            st.push(i);
        }
        
        
        for(int i=0; i<n; i++) 
             cout << left[i] << " : " << right[i] << endl;
        
        int res = 0;
        for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
        {
            long long prod = (left[i]*right[i])%MOD;
            prod = (prod*A[i])%MOD;
            res = (res + prod)%MOD;
        }
        
        return res%MOD;
    }
    
};
```

<a id="problem-920"></a>
## 920. Number of Music Playlists

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/number-of-music-playlists/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1595382766/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-03 09:02 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int numMusicPlaylists(int n, int goal, int k) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        vector<vector<long>> dp(goal + 1, vector<long>(n + 1, 0));
        dp[0][0] = 1;

        for (int i = 1; i <= goal; ++i) {
            for (int j = 1; j <= min(i, n); ++j) {
                dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j - 1] * (n - (j - 1)) % MOD;
                if (j > k)
                    dp[i][j] = (dp[i][j] + dp[i - 1][j] * (j - k) % MOD) % MOD;
            }
        }

        return dp[goal][n];
    }
};
```

**Submitted:** 2025-03-29 14:45 UTC

```cpp
#include <string>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minFlipsMonoIncr(string s) {
        int ones = 0, flips = 0;
        for (char c : s) {
            if (c == '1') ones++;
            else flips = min(flips + 1, ones);
        }
        return flips;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-931"></a>
## 931. Minimum Falling Path Sum

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-falling-path-sum/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1364819748/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-08-22 16:10 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minFallingPathSum(vector<vector<int>>& matrix) {
        int n = matrix.size();

        if(n==1)return matrix[0][0];
        for(int i = n-2;i>=0;i--){
            for(int j =0;j<n;j++){
                if(j==0)matrix[i][j]+= min(matrix[i+1][j],matrix[i+1][j+1]);
                else if(j==n-1)matrix[i][j]+= min(matrix[i+1][j],matrix[i+1][j-1]);
                else{
                    int temp =  min(matrix[i+1][j],matrix[i+1][j+1]);
                    matrix[i][j]+= min(temp,matrix[i+1][j-1]);
                }
            }
        }
        return  *min_element(matrix[0].begin(), matrix[0].end());
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-935"></a>
## 935. Knight Dialer

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/knight-dialer/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1591357517/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-30 18:49 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int knightDialer(int n) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        vector<vector<int>> moves = {{4, 6}, {6, 8}, {7, 9}, {4, 8}, {3, 9, 0}, {}, {1, 7, 0}, {2, 6}, {1, 3}, {2, 4}};
        vector<int> dp(10, 1);

        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            vector<int> temp(10);
            for (int j = 0; j < 10; j++) {
                for (int move : moves[j]) {
                    temp[move] = (temp[move] + dp[j]) % MOD;
                }
            }
            dp = temp;
        }
        return accumulate(dp.begin(), dp.end(), 0L) % MOD;
    }
};
```

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int distinctSubseqII(string s) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        int n = s.size();
        vector<int> dp(n + 1);
        vector<int> last(26, -1);
        dp[0] = 1;

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            dp[i + 1] = (2 * dp[i]) % MOD;
            if (last[s[i] - 'a'] != -1) {
                dp[i + 1] = (dp[i + 1] - dp[last[s[i] - 'a']] + MOD) % MOD;
            }
            last[s[i] - 'a'] = i;
        }

        return (dp[n] - 1 + MOD) % MOD;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-943"></a>
## 943. Find the Shortest Superstring

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/find-the-shortest-superstring/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593698761/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-01 22:49 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <climits>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    string shortestSuperstring(vector<string>& A) {
        int n = A.size();
        vector<vector<int>> overlap(n, vector<int>(n, 0));

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j)
                for (int k = min(A[i].size(), A[j].size()); k >= 0; --k)
                    if (A[i].substr(A[i].size() - k) == A[j].substr(0, k)) {
                        overlap[i][j] = k;
                        break;
                    }

        vector<vector<int>> dp(1 << n, vector<int>(n, INT_MIN));
        vector<vector<int>> parent(1 << n, vector<int>(n, -1));

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
            dp[1 << i][i] = 0;

        for (int mask = 1; mask < (1 << n); ++mask)
            for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
                if (mask & (1 << i))
                    for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j)
                        if (!(mask & (1 << j))) {
                            int nextMask = mask | (1 << j);
                            int val = dp[mask][i] + overlap[i][j];
                            if (val > dp[nextMask][j]) {
                                dp[nextMask][j] = val;
                                parent[nextMask][j] = i;
                            }
                        }

        int last = 0, mask = (1 << n) - 1;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i)
            if (dp[mask][i] > dp[mask][last])
                last = i;

        string res;
        vector<int> path;
        while (last != -1) {
            path.push_back(last);
            int temp = parent[mask][last];
            mask ^= (1 << last);
            last = temp;
        }
        reverse(path.begin(), path.end());

        res += A[path[0]];
        for (int i = 1; i < path.size(); ++i) {
            int o = overlap[path[i - 1]][path[i]];
            res += A[path[i]].substr(o);
        }

        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-956"></a>
## 956. Tallest Billboard

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/tallest-billboard/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1595403002/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-03 09:27 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int tallestBillboard(vector<int>& rods) {
        unordered_map<int, int> dp = {{0, 0}};
        for (int r : rods) {
            auto cur = dp;
            for (auto [d, h] : cur) {
                dp[d + r] = max(dp[d + r], h);
                dp[abs(d - r)] = max(dp[abs(d - r)], h + min(d, r));
            }
        }
        return dp[0];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-960"></a>
## 960. Delete Columns to Make Sorted III

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/delete-columns-to-make-sorted-iii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610605855/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 14:51 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minDeletionSize(vector<string>& strs) {
        int n = strs.size(), m = strs[0].size();
        vector<int> dp(m, 1);
        int res = m - 1;

        for (int j = 1; j < m; ++j)
            for (int i = 0; i < j; ++i) {
                bool valid = true;
                for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k)
                    if (strs[k][i] > strs[k][j]) {
                        valid = false;
                        break;
                    }
                if (valid) dp[j] = max(dp[j], dp[i] + 1);
            }

        return m - *max_element(dp.begin(), dp.end());
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-964"></a>
## 964. Least Operators to Express Number

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/least-operators-to-express-number/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617063513/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:04 UTC

```cpp
// Your solution here
class Solution {
public:
    int leastOpsExpressTarget(int x, int target) {
        // Memoization table to store results for sub-targets
        unordered_map<long long, int> memo;
        
        // Recursive lambda function (DFS with memo) to compute cost for a given value t
        function<int(long long)> dfs = [&](long long t) -> int {
            if (t == 0) {
                return 0;  // 0 can be formed as x - x, but we count that in the caller context
            }
            if (t < x) {
                // If t is smaller than x, choose the cheaper between:
                // adding 1 (x/x) t times vs. subtracting from x.
                int opsAdd = 2 * (int)t - 1;       // cost = t divisions for 1's + (t-1) additions
                int opsSub = 2 * (int)(x - t);    // cost = (x-t) divisions for 1's + (x-t) - 1 additions + 1 subtraction
                return min(opsAdd, opsSub);
            }
            if (memo.count(t)) {
                return memo[t];  // return cached result if already computed
            }
            
            // Find largest power of x (say x^k) that is <= t
            long long p = 1;
            int k = 0;
            while (p * x <= t) {
                p *= x;
                ++k;
            }
            
            if (p == t) {
                // t is exactly x^k, needs k-1 multiplications (e.g., x*x*...*x with k terms has k-1 operators)
                memo[t] = k - 1;
                return k - 1;
            }
            
            // Compute cost if we use p = x^k as part of the expression
            long long remainder = t - p;
            int costUseLower = dfs(remainder) + k;  
            // (k includes k-1 multiplications for x^k and 1 addition to add the remainder)
            
            // Compute cost if we use the next power p*x = x^(k+1) and subtract the excess
            long long leftOver = p * x - t;  // this is the difference we would subtract
            int costUseHigher = INT_MAX;
            if (leftOver < t) {
                costUseHigher = dfs(leftOver) + k + 1;  
                // (k+1 includes k multiplications for x^(k+1) and 1 subtraction operator)
            }
            
            int result = min(costUseLower, costUseHigher);
            memo[t] = result;
            return result;
        };
        
        return dfs(target);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-968"></a>
## 968. Binary Tree Cameras

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/binary-tree-cameras/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1479982257/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-12-16 06:36 UTC

```cpp
/**
 * Definition for a binary tree node.
 * struct TreeNode {
 *     int val;
 *     TreeNode *left;
 *     TreeNode *right;
 *     TreeNode() : val(0), left(nullptr), right(nullptr) {}
 *     TreeNode(int x) : val(x), left(nullptr), right(nullptr) {}
 *     TreeNode(int x, TreeNode *left, TreeNode *right) : val(x), left(left), right(right) {}
 * };
 */
class Solution 
{
public:

    int ans;
    int fun(TreeNode* root) 
    {
        if(!root) return 1;

        int k1 = fun(root->left);
        int k2 = fun(root->right);

        if(k1==0 || k2==0) 
        {
            ans++; 
            return 2;
        }
        if(k1==2 || k2==2)
        {
            return 1;
        }
        
        return 0;

    }
    int minCameraCover(TreeNode* root) {
        ans=0;
        return fun(root)==0 ? ans+1 : ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-975"></a>
## 975. Odd Even Jump

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/odd-even-jump/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1591362358/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-30 18:55 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int oddEvenJumps(vector<int>& arr) {
        int n = arr.size();
        vector<bool> odd(n, false), even(n, false);
        odd[n - 1] = even[n - 1] = true;
        
        map<int, int> indexMap; // Ordered map to store indices
        indexMap[arr[n - 1]] = n - 1;
        int res = 1;

        for (int i = n - 2; i >= 0; --i) {
            auto hi = indexMap.lower_bound(arr[i]);  // Find smallest >= arr[i]
            auto lo = indexMap.upper_bound(arr[i]);  // Find largest < arr[i]
            if (hi != indexMap.end()) odd[i] = even[hi->second]; 
            if (lo != indexMap.begin()) even[i] = odd[prev(lo)->second]; 
            if (odd[i]) res++; 
            
            indexMap[arr[i]] = i;  // Store index for future jumps
        }
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-978"></a>
## 978. Longest Turbulent Subarray

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/longest-turbulent-subarray/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1591368009/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-30 19:03 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maxTurbulenceSize(vector<int>& arr) {
        int inc = 1, dec = 1, maxLen = 1;

        for (int i = 1; i < arr.size(); i++) {
            if (arr[i] > arr[i-1]) {
                inc = dec + 1;
                dec = 1;
            } else if (arr[i] < arr[i-1]) {
                dec = inc + 1;
                inc = 1;
            } else {
                inc = dec = 1;
            }
            maxLen = max(maxLen, max(inc, dec));
        }
        return maxLen;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-983"></a>
## 983. Minimum Cost For Tickets

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-cost-for-tickets/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1536127483/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-02-08 18:52 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    
int mincostTickets(vector<int>& days, vector<int>& costs) {
    // Last travel day
    int lastDay = days.back();
    // Create a set for quick lookup of travel days
    unordered_set<int> travelDays(days.begin(), days.end());
    
    // DP array to store minimum cost up to each day
    vector<int> dp(lastDay + 1, 0);
    
    // Iterate through all days up to the last travel day
    for (int i = 1; i <= lastDay; ++i) {
        if (travelDays.count(i) == 0) {
            // If not a travel day, cost remains the same as the previous day
            dp[i] = dp[i - 1];
        } else {
            // Calculate minimum cost for a travel day
            dp[i] = min({
                dp[i - 1] + costs[0], // 1-day pass
                dp[max(0, i - 7)] + costs[1], // 7-day pass
                dp[max(0, i - 30)] + costs[2] // 30-day pass
            });
        }
    }
    
    // The answer is the cost up to the last travel day
    return dp[lastDay];
}
};
```

<a id="problem-996"></a>
## 996. Number of Squareful Arrays

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/number-of-squareful-arrays/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1595400783/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-03 09:24 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
    int res = 0;

public:
    int numSquarefulPerms(vector<int>& nums) {
        sort(nums.begin(), nums.end());
        vector<bool> used(nums.size(), false);
        backtrack(nums, used, {}, 0);
        return res;
    }

    void backtrack(vector<int>& nums, vector<bool>& used, vector<int> path, int depth) {
        if (depth == nums.size()) {
            res++;
            return;
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < nums.size(); ++i) {
            if (used[i] || (i > 0 && nums[i] == nums[i - 1] && !used[i - 1])) continue;
            if (!path.empty()) {
                int sum = path.back() + nums[i];
                int root = sqrt(sum);
                if (root * root != sum) continue;
            }
            used[i] = true;
            path.push_back(nums[i]);
            backtrack(nums, used, path, depth + 1);
            path.pop_back();
            used[i] = false;
        }
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1000"></a>
## 1000. Minimum Cost to Merge Stones

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-cost-to-merge-stones/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1590162182/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-29 15:12 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <climits>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int mergeStones(vector<int>& stones, int k) {
        int n = stones.size();
        if ((n - 1) % (k - 1) != 0) return -1;

        vector<int> prefix(n + 1, 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) prefix[i + 1] = prefix[i] + stones[i];

        vector<vector<int>> dp(n, vector<int>(n, 0));
        for (int length = k; length <= n; length++) {
            for (int i = 0; i + length <= n; i++) {
                int j = i + length - 1;
                dp[i][j] = INT_MAX;
                for (int m = i; m < j; m += k - 1) {
                    dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j], dp[i][m] + dp[m + 1][j]);
                }
                if ((j - i) % (k - 1) == 0) {
                    dp[i][j] += prefix[j + 1] - prefix[i];
                }
            }
        }
        return dp[0][n - 1];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1012"></a>
## 1012. Numbers With Repeated Digits

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/numbers-with-repeated-digits/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1599283413/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-07 07:02 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <string>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int numDupDigitsAtMostN(int n) {
        string s = to_string(n + 1);
        int len = s.size(), res = 0;

        for (int i = 1; i < len; ++i)
            res += 9 * perm(9, i - 1);

        vector<bool> used(10);
        for (int i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
            int d = s[i] - '0';
            for (int j = i == 0 ? 1 : 0; j < d; ++j)
                if (!used[j])
                    res += perm(9 - i, len - i - 1);
            if (used[d]) break;
            used[d] = true;
        }
        return n - res;
    }

    int perm(int m, int n) {
        int res = 1;
        while (n--) res *= m--;
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1014"></a>
## 1014. Best Sightseeing Pair

**Difficulty:** Medium

**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-30 18:50 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maxScoreSightseeingPair(vector<int>& values) {
        int maxScore = 0;
        int maxVal = values[0];
        for (int i = 1; i < values.size(); i++) {
            maxScore = max(maxScore, maxVal + values[i] - i);
            maxVal = max(maxVal, values[i] + i);
        }
        return maxScore;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1024"></a>
## 1024. Video Stitching

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/video-stitching/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1592317563/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-31 18:01 UTC

```cpp

#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int videoStitching(vector<vector<int>>& clips, int T) {
        sort(clips.begin(), clips.end());
        int end = 0, farthest = 0, count = 0;
        
        for (const auto& clip : clips) {
            if (clip[0] > end) {
                if (clip[0] > farthest) return -1;
                end = farthest;
                count++;
            }
            farthest = max(farthest, clip[1]);
            if (farthest >= T) return count + 1;
        }
        
        return -1;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1027"></a>
## 1027. Longest Arithmetic Subsequence

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/longest-arithmetic-subsequence/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1366786586/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-08-24 14:36 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int longestArithSeqLength(vector<int>& A) {
        
        int n=A.size();
        unordered_map<int,unordered_map<int,int>>dp;
        
        int ans=0;
        for(int i=0;i<n;i++){
            
            for(int j=0;j<i;j++){
                int d=A[i]-A[j];
                if(dp[d].find(j)==dp[d].end()){
                    dp[d][i]=2;
                }
                else dp[d][i]=max(dp[d][j]+1,dp[d][i]);
                
                
                ans=max(ans,dp[d][i]);
            }
        }
        
        
        
        return ans;
        
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1031"></a>
## 1031. Maximum Sum of Two Non-Overlapping Subarrays

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-sum-of-two-non-overlapping-subarrays/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1590142220/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-29 14:57 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maxSumTwoNoOverlap(vector<int>& nums, int L, int M) {
        return max(helper(nums, L, M), helper(nums, M, L));
    }

    int helper(vector<int>& nums, int L, int M) {
        int n = nums.size(), maxL = 0, result = 0;
        vector<int> prefix(n + 1, 0);
        
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
            prefix[i] = prefix[i - 1] + nums[i - 1];
        }
        
        for (int i = L + M; i <= n; i++) {
            maxL = max(maxL, prefix[i - M] - prefix[i - M - L]);
            result = max(result, maxL + prefix[i] - prefix[i - M]);
        }
        
        return result;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1035"></a>
## 1035. Uncrossed Lines

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/uncrossed-lines/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1590138724/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-29 14:55 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maxUncrossedLines(vector<int>& A, vector<int>& B) {
        int m = A.size(), n = B.size();
        vector<vector<int>> dp(m + 1, vector<int>(n + 1, 0));

        for (int i = 1; i <= m; i++) {
            for (int j = 1; j <= n; j++) {
                if (A[i - 1] == B[j - 1]) {
                    dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j - 1] + 1;
                } else {
                    dp[i][j] = max(dp[i - 1][j], dp[i][j - 1]);
                }
            }
        }
        return dp[m][n];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1039"></a>
## 1039. Minimum Score Triangulation of Polygon

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-score-triangulation-of-polygon/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593826013/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-02 02:21 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <climits>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minScoreTriangulation(vector<int>& values) {
        int n = values.size();
        vector<vector<int>> dp(n, vector<int>(n, 0));
        for (int len = 3; len <= n; ++len) {
            for (int i = 0; i + len <= n; ++i) {
                int j = i + len - 1;
                dp[i][j] = INT_MAX;
                for (int k = i + 1; k < j; ++k) {
                    dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j],
                                   dp[i][k] + dp[k][j] + values[i] * values[j] * values[k]);
                }
            }
        }
        return dp[0][n - 1];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1048"></a>
## 1048. Longest String Chain

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/longest-string-chain/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1497897597/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-01-04 23:27 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
    static bool cmp(string &s1,string &s2)
    {
        return s1.length()<s2.length();
    }
    int longestStrChain(vector<string>& words) 
	{
        int ans=0;
        sort(words.begin(),words.end(),cmp);
        map<string,int>mp;
        
        for(string &s:words)
        {
            for(int i=0;i<s.length();i++)
            {
                string r=s.substr(0,i)+s.substr(i+1);
                if(mp.find(r)==mp.end())
                {
                    mp[s]=max(mp[s],1);
                }
                else
                mp[s]=max(mp[s],mp[r]+1);
            }
            ans=max(ans,mp[s]);
        }
        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1049"></a>
## 1049. Last Stone Weight II

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/last-stone-weight-ii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1366530350/  
**Language:** cpp

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int lastStoneWeightII(vector<int>& stones) {
            int sum = 0;
    int total = 0;
    int n = stones.size();
    for(int i = 0;i<n;i++) {
        total+=stones[i];
    }
    sum = total/2;
    vector<vector<int>> dp(n+1,vector<int>(sum+1,0));
    for(int i =1;i<=n;i++) {
        for(int j = 1;j<=sum;j++) {
            if(stones[i-1]>j) {
                dp[i][j] = dp[i-1][j];
            }
            else {
                dp[i][j] = max(dp[i-1][j],dp[i-1][j-stones[i-1]]+stones[i-1]);
            }
        }
    }
    return total-(2*dp[n][sum]);
}
    };
```

<a id="problem-1092"></a>
## 1092. Shortest Common Supersequence 

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/shortest-common-supersequence/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1361606184/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-08-19 17:22 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    void longestCommonSubsequence(string &str1,string &str2,vector<vector<int>>&dp)
    {
        int n = str1.length();
        int m = str2.length();
 
        for(int i = 1;i<=n;i++)
        {
            for(int j = 1;j<=m;j++)
            {
                if(str1[i-1] == str2[j-1])
                {
                    dp[i][j] = 1 + dp[i-1][j-1];
                }
                else
                {
                    dp[i][j] = max(dp[i-1][j],dp[i][j-1]);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    string shortestCommonSupersequence(string str1, string str2) {
        int n = str1.length();
        int m = str2.length();
        vector<vector<int>>dp(n+1,vector<int>(m+1,0));
        longestCommonSubsequence(str1,str2,dp);
 
        int i = n;
        int j = m;
        string str = "";
        while(i > 0 && j > 0)
        {
            if(str1[i-1] == str2[j-1])
            {
                str += str1[i-1];
                i--;
                j--;
            }
            else if(dp[i-1][j] > dp[i][j-1])
            {
                str += str1[i-1];
                i--;
            }
            else
            {
                str += str2[j-1];
                j--;
            }
        }
 
        while(i > 0)
        {
            str += str1[i-1];
            i--;
        }
 
        while(j > 0)
        {
            str += str2[j-1];
            j--;
        }
 
        reverse(str.begin(),str.end());
        return str;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1105"></a>
## 1105. Filling Bookcase Shelves

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/filling-bookcase-shelves/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1591370967/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-30 19:07 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minHeightShelves(vector<vector<int>>& books, int shelfWidth) {
        int n = books.size();
        vector<int> dp(n + 1, 1e9);
        dp[0] = 0;

        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
            int width = 0, height = 0;
            for (int j = i; j > 0; j--) {
                width += books[j - 1][0];
                if (width > shelfWidth) break;
                height = max(height, books[j - 1][1]);
                dp[i] = min(dp[i], dp[j - 1] + height);
            }
        }
        return dp[n];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1125"></a>
## 1125. Smallest Sufficient Team

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/smallest-sufficient-team/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617067453/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:15 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    long long dp[1<<17];
    long long int maxm=(1UL << 61)-1;
    long long helper(unordered_map<string,int>&mp,vector<vector<string>>&people,int mask)
    {
        int sz=mp.size();
        if(mask==((1<<sz)-1))
        {
            return 0ll;
        }
        if(dp[mask]!=-1)return dp[mask];
        long long ans=maxm;
        for(long long i=0;i<(int)people.size();i++)
        {
          
            int newmask=mask;
            long long curr=0;
            for(int j=0;j<(int)people[i].size();j++)
            {
               
                if(mp.count(people[i][j]))
                {
                    int idx=mp[people[i][j]];
                    if((newmask & (1 <<idx ))==0)
                    {newmask=(newmask | (1<<idx));}
                }
            }
            if(newmask > mask)
            {
                curr=(curr | (1UL<<i));
                curr|=helper(mp,people,newmask);
                if(__builtin_popcountll(curr) < __builtin_popcountll(ans))          {
                    ans=curr;
                }
                
            }
        }
        return dp[mask]=ans;
    }
    vector<int> smallestSufficientTeam(vector<string>& req, vector<vector<string>>& people) 
    {
        memset(dp,-1,sizeof(dp));
        unordered_map<string,int>mp;
        for(int i=0;i<(int)req.size();i++)
        mp[req[i]]=i;
        long long ans=helper(mp,people,0);
        vector<int>res;
        for(int i=0;i<=60;i++)
        {
            if(ans & (1UL << i))
            res.push_back(i);
        }
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1130"></a>
## 1130. Minimum Cost Tree From Leaf Values

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-cost-tree-from-leaf-values/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1491044123/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2024-12-28 23:47 UTC

```cpp
class Solution 
{
public:
   int mctFromLeafValues(vector<int>& arr) 
    {
        int sum=0;
        vector<int> v;
        for(int i=0; i<arr.size(); i++)
        {
            while(v.size()!=0 && v.back()<=arr[i])
            {
                if(v.size()>1 && arr[i]>v[v.size()-2])
                    sum+=v.back()*v[v.size()-2];
                else
                    sum+=v.back()*arr[i];
                v.pop_back();
            }
            v.push_back(arr[i]);
        }
        for(int i=v.size()-2; i>=0; i--)
		{
            sum+=v[i]*v[i+1];
		}
        return sum;
    }
};
```
