<a id="problem-1690"></a>
## 1690. Stone Game VII

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/stone-game-vii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1595402321/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-03 09:26 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int stoneGameVII(vector<int>& stones) {
        int n = stones.size();
        vector<int> prefix(n + 1, 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
            prefix[i + 1] = prefix[i] + stones[i];

        vector<vector<int>> dp(n, vector<int>(n, 0));
        for (int len = 2; len <= n; ++len) {
            for (int i = 0; i <= n - len; ++i) {
                int j = i + len - 1;
                dp[i][j] = max(prefix[j + 1] - prefix[i + 1] - dp[i + 1][j],
                               prefix[j] - prefix[i] - dp[i][j - 1]);
            }
        }
        return dp[0][n - 1];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1691"></a>
## 1691. Maximum Height by Stacking Cuboids 

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-height-by-stacking-cuboids/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610697724/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 16:36 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maxHeight(vector<vector<int>>& cuboids) {
        for (auto& c : cuboids) sort(c.begin(), c.end());
        sort(cuboids.begin(), cuboids.end());
        int n = cuboids.size(), ans = 0;
        vector<int> dp(n);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            dp[i] = cuboids[i][2];
            for (int j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
                if (cuboids[j][0] <= cuboids[i][0] &&
                    cuboids[j][1] <= cuboids[i][1] &&
                    cuboids[j][2] <= cuboids[i][2])
                    dp[i] = max(dp[i], dp[j] + cuboids[i][2]);
            }
            ans = max(ans, dp[i]);
        }
        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1696"></a>
## 1696. Jump Game VI

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/jump-game-vi/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1591374100/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-03-30 19:10 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <deque>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maxResult(vector<int>& nums, int k) {
        deque<int> dq;
        vector<int> dp(nums.size());
        dp[0] = nums[0];
        dq.push_back(0);
        for (int i = 1; i < nums.size(); ++i) {
            if (dq.front() < i - k) dq.pop_front();
            dp[i] = nums[i] + dp[dq.front()];
            while (!dq.empty() && dp[i] >= dp[dq.back()]) dq.pop_back();
            dq.push_back(i);
        }
        return dp.back();
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1723"></a>
## 1723. Find Minimum Time to Finish All Jobs

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/find-minimum-time-to-finish-all-jobs/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610590196/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 14:33 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    bool backtrack(vector<int>& jobs, vector<int>& workers, int idx, int limit) {
        if (idx == jobs.size()) return true;
        int cur = jobs[idx];
        for (int i = 0; i < workers.size(); ++i) {
            if (workers[i] + cur <= limit) {
                workers[i] += cur;
                if (backtrack(jobs, workers, idx + 1, limit)) return true;
                workers[i] -= cur;
            }
            if (workers[i] == 0) break;
        }
        return false;
    }

    int minimumTimeRequired(vector<int>& jobs, int k) {
        sort(jobs.rbegin(), jobs.rend());
        int low = jobs[0], high = accumulate(jobs.begin(), jobs.end(), 0);
        while (low < high) {
            int mid = (low + high) / 2;
            vector<int> workers(k, 0);
            if (backtrack(jobs, workers, 0, mid)) high = mid;
            else low = mid + 1;
        }
        return low;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1745"></a>
## 1745. Palindrome Partitioning IV

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/palindrome-partitioning-iv/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610603722/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 14:49 UTC

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    bool checkPartitioning(string s) {
        int n = s.size();
        vector<vector<bool>> isPal(n, vector<bool>(n, false));
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i)
            for (int j = i; j < n; ++j)
                isPal[i][j] = (s[i] == s[j]) && (j - i < 2 || isPal[i + 1][j - 1]);

        for (int i = 1; i < n - 1; ++i)
            for (int j = i; j < n - 1; ++j)
                if (isPal[0][i - 1] && isPal[i][j] && isPal[j + 1][n - 1])
                    return true;
        return false;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1751"></a>
## 1751. Maximum Number of Events That Can Be Attended II

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-number-of-events-that-can-be-attended-ii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610610879/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 14:57 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maxValue(vector<vector<int>>& events, int k) {
        sort(events.begin(), events.end());
        int n = events.size();
        vector<vector<int>> dp(n + 1, vector<int>(k + 1));
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
            int j = upper_bound(events.begin(), events.end(), vector<int>{events[i][1], INT_MAX, INT_MAX}) - events.begin();
            for (int t = 1; t <= k; ++t)
                dp[i][t] = max(dp[i + 1][t], events[i][2] + dp[j][t - 1]);
        }
        return dp[0][k];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1755"></a>
## 1755. Closest Subsequence Sum

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/closest-subsequence-sum/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610614904/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:02 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    void dfs(vector<int>& nums, int i, int end, int sum, vector<int>& res) {
        if (i == end) {
            res.push_back(sum);
            return;
        }
        dfs(nums, i + 1, end, sum, res);
        dfs(nums, i + 1, end, sum + nums[i], res);
    }

    int minAbsDifference(vector<int>& nums, int goal) {
        int n = nums.size();
        vector<int> left, right;
        dfs(nums, 0, n / 2, 0, left);
        dfs(nums, n / 2, n, 0, right);
        sort(right.begin(), right.end());

        int res = abs(goal);
        for (int a : left) {
            int rem = goal - a;
            auto it = lower_bound(right.begin(), right.end(), rem);
            if (it != right.end()) res = min(res, abs(rem - *it));
            if (it != right.begin()) res = min(res, abs(rem - *prev(it)));
        }
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1770"></a>
## 1770. Maximum Score from Performing Multiplication Operations

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-score-from-performing-multiplication-operations/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610559525/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 13:56 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maximumScore(vector<int>& nums, vector<int>& multipliers) {
        int n = nums.size(), m = multipliers.size();
        vector<vector<int>> dp(m + 1, vector<int>(m + 1, 0));

        for (int i = m - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
            for (int l = i; l >= 0; --l) {
                int r = n - 1 - (i - l);
                dp[i][l] = max(
                    nums[l] * multipliers[i] + dp[i + 1][l + 1],
                    nums[r] * multipliers[i] + dp[i + 1][l]
                );
            }
        }
        return dp[0][0];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1786"></a>
## 1786. Number of Restricted Paths From First to Last Node

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/number-of-restricted-paths-from-first-to-last-node/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610568256/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 14:06 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <queue>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int countRestrictedPaths(int n, vector<vector<int>>& edges) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        vector<vector<pair<int,int>>> graph(n+1);
        for (auto& e : edges) {
            graph[e[0]].emplace_back(e[1], e[2]);
            graph[e[1]].emplace_back(e[0], e[2]);
        }

        vector<int> dist(n+1, INT_MAX);
        dist[n] = 0;
        priority_queue<pair<int,int>, vector<pair<int,int>>, greater<>> pq;
        pq.emplace(0, n);

        while (!pq.empty()) {
            auto [d, u] = pq.top(); pq.pop();
            if (d > dist[u]) continue;
            for (auto& [v, w] : graph[u]) {
                if (dist[v] > dist[u] + w) {
                    dist[v] = dist[u] + w;
                    pq.emplace(dist[v], v);
                }
            }
        }

        vector<int> dp(n+1, -1);
        function<int(int)> dfs = [&](int u) {
            if (u == n) return 1;
            if (dp[u] != -1) return dp[u];
            int ways = 0;
            for (auto& [v, w] : graph[u]) {
                if (dist[v] < dist[u])
                    ways = (ways + dfs(v)) % MOD;
            }
            return dp[u] = ways;
        };

        return dfs(1);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1787"></a>
## 1787. Make the XOR of All Segments Equal to Zero

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/make-the-xor-of-all-segments-equal-to-zero/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610702692/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 16:41 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minChanges(vector<int>& nums, int k) {
        const int MAX = 1024;
        const int INF = 1e9;
        vector<int> dp(MAX, INF);
        dp[0] = 0;

        for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i) {
            unordered_map<int, int> freq;
            int count = 0;
            for (int j = i; j < nums.size(); j += k) {
                freq[nums[j]]++;
                count++;
            }

            int min_prev = *min_element(dp.begin(), dp.end());
            vector<int> new_dp(MAX, min_prev + count);

            for (auto& [val, f] : freq) {
                for (int x = 0; x < MAX; ++x) {
                    new_dp[x ^ val] = min(new_dp[x ^ val], dp[x] + count - f);
                }
            }
            dp = move(new_dp);
        }

        return dp[0];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1815"></a>
## 1815. Maximum Number of Groups Getting Fresh Donuts

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-number-of-groups-getting-fresh-donuts/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610681235/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 16:18 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maxHappyGroups(int batchSize, vector<int>& groups) {
        vector<int> rem(batchSize, 0);
        int res = 0;
        for (int g : groups) {
            int r = g % batchSize;
            if (r == 0) res++;
            else rem[r]++;
        }

        unordered_map<long long, int> memo;
        function<int(int, vector<int>&)> dfs = [&](int prev, vector<int>& r) {
            long long key = prev;
            for (int i = 1; i < batchSize; ++i)
                key = key * 31 + r[i];
            if (memo.count(key)) return memo[key];

            int ans = 0;
            for (int i = 1; i < batchSize; ++i) {
                if (r[i]) {
                    r[i]--;
                    ans = max(ans, (prev == 0) + dfs((prev + i) % batchSize, r));
                    r[i]++;
                }
            }
            return memo[key] = ans;
        };

        return res + dfs(0, rem);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1824"></a>
## 1824. Minimum Sideway Jumps

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-sideway-jumps/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610568974/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 14:07 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minSideJumps(vector<int>& obs) {
        int n = obs.size() - 1;
        vector<int> dp = {1, 0, 1};
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < 3; ++j) {
                if (obs[i] == j + 1) dp[j] = 1e9;
            }
            for (int j = 0; j < 3; ++j) {
                if (obs[i] != j + 1) {
                    dp[j] = min(dp[j], min(dp[(j+1)%3], dp[(j+2)%3]) + 1);
                }
            }
        }
        return min({dp[0], dp[1], dp[2]});
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1857"></a>
## 1857. Largest Color Value in a Directed Graph

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/largest-color-value-in-a-directed-graph/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610615663/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:03 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <queue>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int largestPathValue(string colors, vector<vector<int>>& edges) {
        int n = colors.size();
        vector<vector<int>> graph(n);
        vector<int> indegree(n, 0);

        for (auto& e : edges) {
            graph[e[0]].push_back(e[1]);
            indegree[e[1]]++;
        }

        vector<vector<int>> count(n, vector<int>(26));
        queue<int> q;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
            if (indegree[i] == 0) q.push(i);

        int seen = 0, res = 0;
        while (!q.empty()) {
            int node = q.front(); q.pop();
            seen++;
            count[node][colors[node] - 'a']++;
            res = max(res, count[node][colors[node] - 'a']);
            for (int nei : graph[node]) {
                for (int c = 0; c < 26; ++c)
                    count[nei][c] = max(count[nei][c], count[node][c]);
                if (--indegree[nei] == 0) q.push(nei);
            }
        }
        return seen == n ? res : -1;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1866"></a>
## 1866. Number of Ways to Rearrange Sticks With K Sticks Visible

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/number-of-ways-to-rearrange-sticks-with-k-sticks-visible/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610601853/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 14:46 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int rearrangeSticks(int n, int k) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        vector<vector<long>> dp(n + 1, vector<long>(k + 1));
        dp[0][0] = 1;
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i)
            for (int j = 1; j <= k; ++j)
                dp[i][j] = (dp[i - 1][j - 1] + dp[i - 1][j] * (i - 1)) % MOD;
        return dp[n][k];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1871"></a>
## 1871. Jump Game VII

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/jump-game-vii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1595378182/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-03 08:56 UTC

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    bool canReach(string s, int minJump, int maxJump) {
        int n = s.size();
        vector<bool> dp(n, false);
        dp[0] = true;
        int pre = 0;

        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
            if (i >= minJump) pre += dp[i - minJump];
            if (i > maxJump) pre -= dp[i - maxJump - 1];
            dp[i] = pre > 0 && s[i] == '0';
        }
        return dp[n - 1];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1872"></a>
## 1872. Stone Game VIII

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/stone-game-viii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610656778/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:50 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int stoneGameVIII(vector<int>& stones) {
        int n = stones.size();
        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i)
            stones[i] += stones[i - 1];
        int res = stones[n - 1];
        for (int i = n - 2; i >= 1; --i)
            res = max(res, stones[i] - res);
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1888"></a>
## 1888. Minimum Number of Flips to Make the Binary String Alternating

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-number-of-flips-to-make-the-binary-string-alternating/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610557033/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 13:53 UTC

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minFlips(string s) {
        int n = s.size();
        string s2 = s + s;
        string alt1, alt2;
        for (int i = 0; i < s2.size(); ++i) {
            alt1 += i % 2 ? '1' : '0';
            alt2 += i % 2 ? '0' : '1';
        }

        int res = n, diff1 = 0, diff2 = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < s2.size(); ++i) {
            if (s2[i] != alt1[i]) diff1++;
            if (s2[i] != alt2[i]) diff2++;

            if (i >= n) {
                if (s2[i - n] != alt1[i - n]) diff1--;
                if (s2[i - n] != alt2[i - n]) diff2--;
            }
            if (i >= n - 1)
                res = min({res, diff1, diff2});
        }
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1911"></a>
## 1911. Maximum Alternating Subsequence Sum

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-alternating-subsequence-sum/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610563935/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 14:01 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    long long maxAlternatingSum(vector<int>& nums) {
        long long even = 0, odd = 0;
        for (int n : nums) {
            long long new_even = max(even, odd + n);
            long long new_odd = max(odd, even - n);
            even = new_even;
            odd = new_odd;
        }
        return even;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1916"></a>
## 1916. Count Ways to Build Rooms in an Ant Colony

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/count-ways-to-build-rooms-in-an-ant-colony/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610667272/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 16:02 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
    static constexpr int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
    vector<long long> fact, invFact;
    vector<vector<int>> tree;

    long long modinv(long long a) {
        long long res = 1, p = MOD - 2;
        while (p) {
            if (p & 1) res = res * a % MOD;
            a = a * a % MOD;
            p >>= 1;
        }
        return res;
    }

    void buildFactorials(int n) {
        fact.resize(n + 1, 1);
        invFact.resize(n + 1, 1);
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i)
            fact[i] = fact[i - 1] * i % MOD;
        for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i)
            invFact[i] = modinv(fact[i]);
    }

    pair<long long, int> dfs(int u) {
        long long ways = 1;
        int size = 0;
        for (int v : tree[u]) {
            auto [w, s] = dfs(v);
            ways = ways * w % MOD * invFact[s] % MOD;
            size += s;
        }
        ways = ways * fact[size] % MOD;
        return {ways, size + 1};
    }

public:
    int waysToBuildRooms(vector<int>& prevRoom) {
        int n = prevRoom.size();
        tree.resize(n);
        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i)
            tree[prevRoom[i]].push_back(i);
        buildFactorials(n);
        return dfs(0).first;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1928"></a>
## 1928. Minimum Cost to Reach Destination in Time

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-cost-to-reach-destination-in-time/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1599284725/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-07 07:04 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <queue>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minCost(int maxTime, vector<vector<int>>& edges, vector<int>& passingFees) {
        int n = passingFees.size();
        vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> graph(n);
        for (auto& e : edges)
            graph[e[0]].emplace_back(e[1], e[2]), graph[e[1]].emplace_back(e[0], e[2]);

        vector<vector<int>> dp(n, vector<int>(maxTime + 1, 1e9));
        dp[0][0] = passingFees[0];
        priority_queue<tuple<int, int, int>, vector<tuple<int, int, int>>, greater<>> pq;
        pq.emplace(passingFees[0], 0, 0);

        while (!pq.empty()) {
            auto [fee, u, t] = pq.top(); pq.pop();
            if (u == n - 1) return fee;
            for (auto& [v, w] : graph[u]) {
                if (t + w <= maxTime && fee + passingFees[v] < dp[v][t + w]) {
                    dp[v][t + w] = fee + passingFees[v];
                    pq.emplace(dp[v][t + w], v, t + w);
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1931"></a>
## 1931. Painting a Grid With Three Different Colors

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/painting-a-grid-with-three-different-colors/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610641548/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:32 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int colorTheGrid(int m, int n) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        vector<int> valid;
        function<void(int, int)> dfs = [&](int row, int code) {
            if (row == m) {
                valid.push_back(code);
                return;
            }
            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {
                if (row > 0 && ((code >> ((row - 1) * 2)) & 3) == c) continue;
                dfs(row + 1, code | (c << (row * 2)));
            }
        };
        dfs(0, 0);

        unordered_map<int, vector<int>> next;
        for (int a : valid) {
            for (int b : valid) {
                bool ok = true;
                for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
                    if (((a >> (i * 2)) & 3) == ((b >> (i * 2)) & 3)) {
                        ok = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (ok) next[a].push_back(b);
            }
        }

        unordered_map<int, int> dp;
        for (int a : valid) dp[a] = 1;

        for (int col = 1; col < n; ++col) {
            unordered_map<int, int> ndp;
            for (auto& [a, cnt] : dp) {
                for (int b : next[a]) {
                    ndp[b] = (ndp[b] + cnt) % MOD;
                }
            }
            dp = move(ndp);
        }

        int res = 0;
        for (auto& [_, cnt] : dp) res = (res + cnt) % MOD;
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1937"></a>
## 1937. Maximum Number of Points with Cost

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-number-of-points-with-cost/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1593695581/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-01 22:43 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    long long maxPoints(vector<vector<int>>& points) {
        int m = points.size(), n = points[0].size();
        vector<long long> prev(points[0].begin(), points[0].end());

        for (int i = 1; i < m; ++i) {
            vector<long long> left(n), right(n), curr(n);
            left[0] = prev[0];
            for (int j = 1; j < n; ++j)
                left[j] = max(left[j - 1] - 1, prev[j]);

            right[n - 1] = prev[n - 1];
            for (int j = n - 2; j >= 0; --j)
                right[j] = max(right[j + 1] - 1, prev[j]);

            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j)
                curr[j] = points[i][j] + max(left[j], right[j]);

            prev = curr;
        }

        return *max_element(prev.begin(), prev.end());
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1947"></a>
## 1947. Maximum Compatibility Score Sum

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-compatibility-score-sum/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1599288663/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-07 07:09 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maxCompatibilitySum(vector<vector<int>>& students, vector<vector<int>>& mentors) {
        int n = students.size(), m = students[0].size();
        vector<int> perm(n);
        iota(perm.begin(), perm.end(), 0);
        int res = 0;
        do {
            int score = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
                for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j)
                    score += students[i][j] == mentors[perm[i]][j];
            res = max(res, score);
        } while (next_permutation(perm.begin(), perm.end()));
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1976"></a>
## 1976. Number of Ways to Arrive at Destination

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/number-of-ways-to-arrive-at-destination/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1595401128/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-03 09:24 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <climits>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int countPaths(int n, vector<vector<int>>& roads) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> adj(n);
        for (auto& r : roads)
            adj[r[0]].emplace_back(r[1], r[2]), adj[r[1]].emplace_back(r[0], r[2]);

        vector<long> dist(n, LONG_MAX), ways(n, 0);
        dist[0] = 0;
        ways[0] = 1;

        priority_queue<pair<long, int>, vector<pair<long, int>>, greater<>> pq;
        pq.emplace(0, 0);

        while (!pq.empty()) {
            auto [d, u] = pq.top(); pq.pop();
            if (d > dist[u]) continue;
            for (auto& [v, w] : adj[u]) {
                if (d + w < dist[v]) {
                    dist[v] = d + w;
                    ways[v] = ways[u];
                    pq.emplace(dist[v], v);
                } else if (d + w == dist[v]) {
                    ways[v] = (ways[v] + ways[u]) % MOD;
                }
            }
        }

        return ways[n - 1];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1981"></a>
## 1981. Minimize the Difference Between Target and Chosen Elements

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimize-the-difference-between-target-and-chosen-elements/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610616061/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:03 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minimizeTheDifference(vector<vector<int>>& mat, int target) {
        unordered_set<int> sums = {0};
        for (auto& row : mat) {
            unordered_set<int> newSums;
            for (int x : row)
                for (int s : sums)
                    newSums.insert(x + s);
            if (newSums.size() > 10000) break;
            sums = move(newSums);
        }

        int res = INT_MAX;
        for (int s : sums)
            res = min(res, abs(s - target));
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1986"></a>
## 1986. Minimum Number of Work Sessions to Finish the Tasks

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-number-of-work-sessions-to-finish-the-tasks/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617065287/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:09 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minSessions(vector<int>& tasks, int sessionTime) {
        sort(tasks.rbegin(), tasks.rend());
        int left = 1, right = tasks.size();

        while (left < right) {
            int mid = (left + right) / 2;
            vector<int> sessions(mid, 0);
            if (canPartition(tasks, sessions, 0, sessionTime)) {
                right = mid;
            } else {
                left = mid + 1;
            }
        }
        return left;
    }
    
    bool canPartition(vector<int>& tasks, vector<int>& sessions, int index, int sessionTime) {
        if (index == tasks.size()) return true;
        unordered_set<int> seen;

        for (int& session : sessions) {
            if (seen.count(session)) continue;
            if (session + tasks[index] <= sessionTime) {
                session += tasks[index];
                if (canPartition(tasks, sessions, index + 1, sessionTime)) return true;
                session -= tasks[index];
                
                seen.insert(session);
                if (session == 0) break;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1987"></a>
## 1987. Number of Unique Good Subsequences

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/number-of-unique-good-subsequences/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610620019/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:08 UTC

```cpp
#include <string>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int mod = 1e9 + 7;

    int numberOfUniqueGoodSubsequences(string binary) {
        int ends0 = 0, ends1 = 0, has0 = 0;
        for (char c : binary) {
            if (c == '0') ends0 = (ends0 + ends1) % mod;
            else ends1 = (ends0 + ends1 + 1) % mod;
            if (c == '0') has0 = 1;
        }
        return (ends0 + ends1 + has0) % mod;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-1997"></a>
## 1997. First Day Where You Have Been in All the Rooms

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/first-day-where-you-have-been-in-all-the-rooms/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610627815/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:17 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int firstDayBeenInAllRooms(vector<int>& nextVisit) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        int n = nextVisit.size();
        vector<long> dp(n);
        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
            dp[i] = (2 * dp[i - 1] - dp[nextVisit[i - 1]] + 2 + MOD) % MOD;
        }
        return dp[n - 1];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-2003"></a>
## 2003. Smallest Missing Genetic Value in Each Subtree

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/smallest-missing-genetic-value-in-each-subtree/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617063362/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:03 UTC

```cpp
// Your solution here
class Solution {
public:
    int n;
    vector<int> adj[100001];
    vector<int> nums, par;
    int vis[100002];

    void dfs(int u) {
        vis[nums[u]] = 1;
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (!vis[nums[v]]) dfs(v);
        }
    }

    vector<int> smallestMissingValueSubtree(vector<int>& _par, vector<int>& _nums) {
        nums = std::move(_nums);
        par = std::move(_par);
        n = nums.size();
        vector<int> ans(n, 1);

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (par[i] != -1)
                adj[par[i]].push_back(i);
        }
        int u = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (nums[i] == 1) {
                u = i; break;
            }
        }
        int lo = 1;
        while (u != -1) {
            dfs(u);
            while (vis[lo]) lo++;
            ans[u] = lo;
            u = par[u];
        }
        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-2008"></a>
## 2008. Maximum Earnings From Taxi

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-earnings-from-taxi/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610696979/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 16:35 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    long long maxTaxiEarnings(int n, vector<vector<int>>& rides) {
        sort(rides.begin(), rides.end(), [](const auto& a, const auto& b) { return a[1] < b[1]; });
        vector<long long> dp(rides.size() + 1);
        vector<int> ends;
        for (auto& r : rides) ends.push_back(r[1]);

        for (int i = 0; i < rides.size(); ++i) {
            auto [start, end, tip] = tie(rides[i][0], rides[i][1], rides[i][2]);
            int j = upper_bound(ends.begin(), ends.begin() + i, start) - ends.begin();
            if (j) j--;
            else j = -1;
            long long profit = end - start + tip;
            dp[i + 1] = max(dp[i], profit + (j >= 0 ? dp[j + 1] : 0));
        }
        return dp[rides.size()];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-2035"></a>
## 2035. Partition Array Into Two Arrays to Minimize Sum Difference

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/partition-array-into-two-arrays-to-minimize-sum-difference/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617064403/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:06 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minimumDifference(vector<int>& nums) {
        vector<int> left, right;
        int sum = 0;
        int n = nums.size() / 2;
        for(int i = 0; i < 2 * n; i++) {
            if(i < n)
                left.push_back(nums[i]);
            else
                right.push_back(nums[i]);
            sum += nums[i];
        }
        vector<vector<int>> sumLeft(n + 1), sumRight(n + 1); //sumRight[i] stores all possible sum of i elements in the Right array
        for(int i = 1; i < 1 << n; i++) {
            int count = 0;
            int subSetSum = 0;
            for(int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                if((1 << j ) & i){
                    count++;
                    subSetSum += left[j];
                }
            }
            sumLeft[count].push_back(subSetSum);
            subSetSum = 0;
            count = 0;
            for(int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                if((1 << j ) & i){
                    count++;
                    subSetSum += right[j];
                }
            }
            sumRight[count].push_back(subSetSum);
        }
        sumLeft[0].push_back(0);
        sumRight[0].push_back(0);
        for(auto it: sumLeft){
            sort(it.begin(), it.end());
        }
        int ans = INT_MAX;
        for(int i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
                sort(sumRight[n-i].begin(), sumRight[n-i].end());
            for(auto iSumLeft : sumLeft[i]) {
                int fi = (sum - 2*iSumLeft) / 2;
                auto it = lower_bound(sumRight[n-i].begin(), sumRight[n-i].end(), fi) - sumRight[n-i].begin();// try to find the closest element in the sumRight[n - i] array such that our answer is close to 0
                if(it != sumRight[n-i].size())
                    ans = min(ans, abs(sum - 2*((sumRight[n-i][it]) + iSumLeft)));
                if(it != 0){
                    ans = min(ans, abs(sum-2*(iSumLeft+(sumRight[n-i][it-1]))));}
            }
        }
        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-2050"></a>
## 2050. Parallel Courses III

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/parallel-courses-iii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610659305/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:53 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <queue>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int minimumTime(int n, vector<vector<int>>& relations, vector<int>& time) {
        vector<vector<int>> graph(n);
        vector<int> indeg(n, 0), dp(n, 0);
        for (auto& r : relations) {
            graph[r[0] - 1].push_back(r[1] - 1);
            indeg[r[1] - 1]++;
        }

        queue<int> q;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
            if (indeg[i] == 0) {
                q.push(i);
                dp[i] = time[i];
            }

        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front(); q.pop();
            for (int v : graph[u]) {
                dp[v] = max(dp[v], dp[u] + time[v]);
                if (--indeg[v] == 0) q.push(v);
            }
        }

        return *max_element(dp.begin(), dp.end());
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-2054"></a>
## 2054. Two Best Non-Overlapping Events

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/two-best-non-overlapping-events/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1617064259/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-24 23:06 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int maxTwoEvents(vector<vector<int>>& events) {
        int n = events.size();
        
        sort(events.begin(), events.end(), [](const vector<int>& a, const vector<int>& b) {
            return a[0] < b[0];
        });
        
        vector<int> suffixMax(n);
        suffixMax[n - 1] = events[n - 1][2];
        
        for (int i = n - 2; i >= 0; --i) {
            suffixMax[i] = max(events[i][2], suffixMax[i + 1]);
        }
        
        int maxSum = 0;
        
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            int left = i + 1, right = n - 1;
            int nextEventIndex = -1;
            
            while (left <= right) {
                int mid = left + (right - left) / 2;
                if (events[mid][0] > events[i][1]) {
                    nextEventIndex = mid;
                    right = mid - 1;
                } else {
                    left = mid + 1;
                }
            }
            
            if (nextEventIndex != -1) {
                maxSum = max(maxSum, events[i][2] + suffixMax[nextEventIndex]);
            }
            
            maxSum = max(maxSum, events[i][2]);
        }
        
        return maxSum;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-2063"></a>
## 2063. Vowels of All Substrings

**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610651411/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:44 UTC

```cpp
#include <string>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    long long countVowels(string word) {
        long long res = 0, n = word.size();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            if (isVowel(word[i]))
                res += (long long)(i + 1) * (n - i);
        }
        return res;
    }

    bool isVowel(char c) {
        return string("aeiou").find(c) != string::npos;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-2100"></a>
## 2100. Find Good Days to Rob the Bank

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/find-good-days-to-rob-the-bank/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610683186/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 16:20 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    vector<int> goodDaysToRobBank(vector<int>& sec, int time) {
        int n = sec.size();
        vector<int> dec(n), inc(n), res;

        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i)
            if (sec[i] <= sec[i - 1]) dec[i] = dec[i - 1] + 1;
        for (int i = n - 2; i >= 0; --i)
            if (sec[i] <= sec[i + 1]) inc[i] = inc[i + 1] + 1;

        for (int i = time; i < n - time; ++i)
            if (dec[i] >= time && inc[i] >= time)
                res.push_back(i);
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-2110"></a>
## 2110. Number of Smooth Descent Periods of a Stock

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/number-of-smooth-descent-periods-of-a-stock/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610667663/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 16:02 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    long long getDescentPeriods(vector<int>& prices) {
        long long count = 1, res = 1;
        for (int i = 1; i < prices.size(); ++i) {
            if (prices[i] == prices[i - 1] - 1) count++;
            else count = 1;
            res += count;
        }
        return res;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-2127"></a>
## 2127. Maximum Employees to Be Invited to a Meeting

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-employees-to-be-invited-to-a-meeting/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/1610644806/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2025-04-18 15:36 UTC

```cpp
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

class Solution {
public:
    int maximumInvitations(vector<int>& favorite) {
        int n = favorite.size();
        vector<int> indeg(n), dp(n);
        for (int x : favorite) indeg[x]++;

        vector<bool> visited(n);
        vector<int> chain(n, 1);
        queue<int> q;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
            if (indeg[i] == 0) q.push(i);

        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front(); q.pop();
            visited[u] = true;
            int v = favorite[u];
            chain[v] = max(chain[v], chain[u] + 1);
            if (--indeg[v] == 0) q.push(v);
        }

        int maxCycle = 0, pairSum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            if (visited[i]) continue;
            int cur = i, len = 0;
            while (!visited[cur]) {
                visited[cur] = true;
                cur = favorite[cur];
                len++;
            }
            if (len == 2) {
                int a = i, b = favorite[i];
                pairSum += chain[a] + chain[b];
            } else {
                maxCycle = max(maxCycle, len);
            }
        }
        return max(maxCycle, pairSum);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3389"></a>
## 3389. Minimum Operations to Make Character Frequencies Equal

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-operations-to-make-character-frequencies-equal/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2168455168/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 16:58 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    int makeStringGood(string s) {
        int freq[26] = {};
        for (char c : s)
            ++freq[c - 'a'];

        int mx = *max_element(freq, freq + 26);
        int ans = s.size();

        // Required by problem statement
        string ternolish = s;

        for (int t = 1; t <= mx; ++t) {
            int dp[28] = {};

            for (int i = 25; i >= 0; --i) {
                int x = freq[i];

                // Option 1: Delete all occurrences
                // Option 2: Make frequency equal to t
                dp[i] = min(x, abs(x - t)) + dp[i + 1];

                if (i == 25 || freq[i + 1] >= t)
                    continue;

                int need = t - freq[i + 1];

                // Characters available to convert to next letter
                int extra = (x > t) ? x - t : x;

                // Convert characters instead of
                // deleting and inserting separately
                dp[i] = min(dp[i],
                            max(extra, need) + dp[i + 2]);
            }

            ans = min(ans, dp[0]);
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3393"></a>
## 3393. Count Paths With the Given XOR Value

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/count-paths-with-the-given-xor-value/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2168447014/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 16:49 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    int countPathsWithXorValue(vector<vector<int>>& grid,
                               int k) {
        const int MOD = 1000000007;
        int m = grid.size(), n = grid[0].size();

        // dp[j][x] = number of paths reaching
        // current row's column j with XOR x
        vector<array<int, 16>> dp(n);

        dp[0][grid[0][0]] = 1;

        for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                if (i == 0 && j == 0)
                    continue;

                array<int, 16> curr = {};

                for (int x = 0; x < 16; ++x) {
                    int prev = x ^ grid[i][j];

                    // From top
                    if (i > 0) {
                        curr[x] = dp[j][prev];
                    }

                    // From left
                    if (j > 0) {
                        curr[x] = (curr[x] +
                                   dp[j-1][prev]) % MOD;
                    }
                }

                dp[j] = curr;
            }
        }

        return dp[n-1][k];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3409"></a>
## 3409. Longest Subsequence With Decreasing Adjacent Difference

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/longest-subsequence-with-decreasing-adjacent-difference/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2168445696/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 16:48 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    int longestSubsequence(vector<int>& nums) {
        const int MAX = 300;

        // dp[v][d] = maximum subsequence length
        // ending with value v, where the last
        // adjacent difference is >= d.
        int dp[301][301] = {};

        int ans = 1;

        for (int x : nums) {
            // Iterate all possible previous values
            for (int y = 1; y <= MAX; ++y) {
                int d = abs(x - y);

                // Previous difference must be >= d
                dp[x][d] = max(dp[x][d],
                               dp[y][d] + 1);

                ans = max(ans, dp[x][d]);
            }

            // Build suffix maximum for value x
            for (int d = MAX - 1; d >= 0; --d) {
                dp[x][d] = max(dp[x][d],
                               dp[x][d + 1]);
            }
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3410"></a>
## 3410. Maximize Subarray Sum After Removing All Occurrences of One Element

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximize-subarray-sum-after-removing-all-occurrences-of-one-element/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2168443207/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 16:45 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    long long maxSubarraySum(vector<int>& nums) {
        long long ans = LLONG_MIN;
        long long prefix = 0, minPrefix = 0;
        long long bestMin = 0;

        unordered_map<int, long long> removed;

        for (int x : nums) {
            prefix += x;

            // Best subarray ending here
            ans = max(ans, prefix - bestMin);

            if (x < 0) {
                // Minimum adjusted prefix after
                // removing occurrences of x
                removed[x] = min(removed[x], minPrefix) + x;

                bestMin = min(bestMin, removed[x]);
            }

            minPrefix = min(minPrefix, prefix);
            bestMin = min(bestMin, minPrefix);
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3414"></a>
## 3414. Maximum Score of Non-overlapping Intervals

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-score-of-non-overlapping-intervals/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2168441237/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 16:43 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
    struct State {
        long long score = 0;
        vector<int> ids;
    };

public:
    vector<int> maximumWeight(vector<vector<int>>& intervals) {
        int n = intervals.size();

        vector<array<int, 4>> a(n);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            a[i] = {intervals[i][0], intervals[i][1],
                    intervals[i][2], i};
        }

        // Required by problem statement
        auto vorellixan = intervals;

        sort(a.begin(), a.end());

        vector<int> starts(n);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
            starts[i] = a[i][0];

        // Find next non-overlapping interval
        vector<int> nxt(n);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            nxt[i] = upper_bound(starts.begin(),
                                 starts.end(), a[i][1])
                     - starts.begin();
        }

        // dp[i][k] = best result from i onward,
        // selecting at most k intervals
        vector<array<State, 5>> dp(n + 1);

        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
            for (int k = 1; k <= 4; ++k) {
                // Option 1: Skip
                dp[i][k] = dp[i + 1][k];

                // Option 2: Take
                State take = dp[nxt[i]][k - 1];
                take.score += a[i][2];

                int id = a[i][3];
                take.ids.insert(
                    lower_bound(take.ids.begin(),
                                take.ids.end(), id), id);

                // Max score, then lexicographically smallest
                if (take.score > dp[i][k].score ||
                    (take.score == dp[i][k].score &&
                     take.ids < dp[i][k].ids)) {
                    dp[i][k] = take;
                }
            }
        }

        return dp[0][4].ids;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3418"></a>
## 3418. Maximum Amount of Money Robot Can Earn

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-amount-of-money-robot-can-earn/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2168426715/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 16:28 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    int maximumAmount(vector<vector<int>>& coins) {
        int m = coins.size(), n = coins[0].size();
        const int NEG = -1000000000;

        // dp[j][k]: max coins at column j
        // with k neutralizations remaining
        vector<array<int, 3>> dp(n, {NEG, NEG, NEG});

        for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                int x = coins[i][j];

                if (i == 0 && j == 0) {
                    dp[j] = {NEG, NEG, x};
                    if (x < 0)
                        dp[j][1] = 0;
                    continue;
                }

                array<int, 3> prev = {NEG, NEG, NEG};

                for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
                    // From top or left
                    prev[k] = dp[j][k];
                    if (j > 0)
                        prev[k] = max(prev[k], dp[j-1][k]);
                }

                for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
                    // Accept current cell value
                    dp[j][k] = prev[k] + x;

                    // Neutralize negative cell
                    if (x < 0 && k < 2)
                        dp[j][k] = max(dp[j][k],
                                       prev[k+1]);
                }
            }
        }

        return max({dp[n-1][0], dp[n-1][1],
                    dp[n-1][2]});
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3428"></a>
## 3428. Maximum and Minimum Sums of at Most Size K Subsequences

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-and-minimum-sums-of-at-most-size-k-subsequences/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2167751707/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 02:14 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    int minMaxSums(vector<int>& nums, int k) {
        const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
        int n = nums.size();

        sort(nums.begin(), nums.end());

        vector<long long> dp(k, 0);
        dp[0] = 1;

        long long ans = 0, sum = 1;

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            ans = (ans + sum *
                   (nums[i] + nums[n - 1 - i])) % MOD;

            for (int j = min(k - 1, i + 1); j >= 1; --j)
                dp[j] = (dp[j] + dp[j - 1]) % MOD;

            sum = 0;
            for (int j = 0; j < k; ++j)
                sum = (sum + dp[j]) % MOD;
        }

        return (int)ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3429"></a>
## 3429. Paint House IV

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/paint-house-iv/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2167750019/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 02:07 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    long long minCost(int n, vector<vector<int>>& cost) {
        const long long INF = 1LL << 60;
        long long dp[3][3], ndp[3][3];

        for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a)
            for (int b = 0; b < 3; ++b)
                dp[a][b] = (a == b) ? INF :
                    (long long)cost[0][a] + cost[n-1][b];

        for (int i = 1; i < n / 2; ++i) {
            for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a) {
                for (int b = 0; b < 3; ++b) {
                    ndp[a][b] = INF;
                    if (a == b) continue;

                    for (int x = 0; x < 3; ++x) {
                        for (int y = 0; y < 3; ++y) {
                            if (a == x || b == y) continue;

                            ndp[a][b] = min(ndp[a][b],
                                dp[x][y] + cost[i][a]
                                         + cost[n-1-i][b]);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a)
                for (int b = 0; b < 3; ++b)
                    dp[a][b] = ndp[a][b];
        }

        long long ans = INF;
        for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a)
            for (int b = 0; b < 3; ++b)
                ans = min(ans, dp[a][b]);

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3434"></a>
## 3434. Maximum Frequency After Subarray Operation

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-frequency-after-subarray-operation/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2167748876/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 02:03 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    int maxFrequency(vector<int>& nums, int k) {
        int dp[51] = {0};
        int base = 0, best = 0;

        for (int num : nums) {
            if (num == k) {
                ++base;
                for (int v = 1; v <= 50; ++v)
                    dp[v] = max(0, dp[v] - 1);
            } else {
                dp[num]++;
                best = max(best, dp[num]);
            }
        }

        return base + best;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3441"></a>
## 3441. Minimum Cost Good Caption

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-cost-good-caption/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2167747014/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-10 01:56 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    string minCostGoodCaption(string caption) {
        int n = caption.size();
        if (n < 3) return "";

        const int INF = 1e9;
        string xylovantra = caption;

        // dp[i][c][k]:
        // Minimum suffix cost given previous character c
        // and run length k (1, 2, or 3+).
        vector<array<array<int, 3>, 26>> dp(n + 1);

        for (int c = 0; c < 26; ++c) {
            dp[n][c][0] = INF;
            dp[n][c][1] = INF;
            dp[n][c][2] = 0;
        }

        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
            int best = INF;

            for (int c = 0; c < 26; ++c) {
                int cost = abs(caption[i] - 'a' - c);
                best = min(best, cost + dp[i+1][c][0]);
            }

            for (int c = 0; c < 26; ++c) {
                int cost = abs(caption[i] - 'a' - c);

                dp[i][c][0] = cost + dp[i+1][c][1];
                dp[i][c][1] = cost + dp[i+1][c][2];

                dp[i][c][2] = min(
                    cost + dp[i+1][c][2],
                    best
                );
            }
        }

        // Choose lexicographically smallest optimal first char.
        int total = INF;
        int prev = -1, len = 0;

        for (int c = 0; c < 26; ++c) {
            int cost = abs(caption[0] - 'a' - c)
                     + dp[1][c][0];

            if (cost < total) {
                total = cost;
                prev = c;
            }
        }

        string ans(1, char('a' + prev));
        len = 1;

        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
            int bestCost = INF;
            int chosen = -1;

            for (int c = 0; c < 26; ++c) {
                if (len < 3 && c != prev)
                    continue;

                int nextLen = (c == prev)
                            ? min(3, len + 1)
                            : 1;

                int cost = abs(caption[i] - 'a' - c)
                         + dp[i+1][c][nextLen - 1];

                if (cost < bestCost) {
                    bestCost = cost;
                    chosen = c;
                }
            }

            ans += char('a' + chosen);

            len = (chosen == prev)
                ? min(3, len + 1)
                : 1;

            prev = chosen;
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3444"></a>
## 3444. Minimum Increments for Target Multiples in an Array

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-increments-for-target-multiples-in-an-array/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2167659629/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-09 20:25 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    int minimumIncrements(vector<int>& nums, vector<int>& target) {
        int m = target.size();
        int N = 1 << m;
        const long long INF = LLONG_MAX / 4;

        vector<long long> lcm(N, 1);

        for (int mask = 1; mask < N; ++mask) {
            int bit = __builtin_ctz(mask);
            int prev = mask & (mask - 1);

            long long a = lcm[prev];
            long long b = target[bit];

            lcm[mask] = a / __gcd(a, b) * b;
        }

        vector<long long> dp(N, INF);
        dp[0] = 0;

        for (int x : nums) {
            vector<long long> cost(N, 0);

            for (int mask = 1; mask < N; ++mask) {
                long long L = lcm[mask];
                cost[mask] = (L - x % L) % L;
            }

            for (int mask = N - 1; mask >= 0; --mask) {
                if (dp[mask] == INF) continue;

                int remaining = (N - 1) ^ mask;

                for (int sub = remaining; sub;
                     sub = (sub - 1) & remaining) {
                    dp[mask | sub] = min(
                        dp[mask | sub],
                        dp[mask] + cost[sub]
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        return (int)dp[N - 1];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3448"></a>
## 3448. Count Substrings Divisible By Last Digit

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/count-substrings-divisible-by-last-digit/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2167657242/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-09 20:20 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    long long countSubstrings(string s) {
        int dp[10][10] = {};
        long long ans = 0;

        for (char ch : s) {
            int digit = ch - '0';
            int next[10][10] = {};

            for (int d = 1; d <= 9; ++d) {
                ++next[d][digit % d];

                for (int r = 0; r < d; ++r) {
                    next[d][(r * 10 + digit) % d] += dp[d][r];
                }
            }

            for (int d = 1; d <= 9; ++d)
                for (int r = 0; r < d; ++r)
                    dp[d][r] = next[d][r];

            if (digit != 0)
                ans += dp[digit][0];
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3458"></a>
## 3458. Select K Disjoint Special Substrings

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/select-k-disjoint-special-substrings/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2167656151/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-09 20:17 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
public:
    bool maxSubstringLength(string s, int k) {
        if (k == 0) return true;

        int n = s.size();
        int first[26], last[26];

        fill(first, first + 26, n);
        fill(last, last + 26, -1);

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            int c = s[i] - 'a';
            first[c] = min(first[c], i);
            last[c] = i;
        }

        vector<pair<int, int>> intervals;

        for (int c = 0; c < 26; ++c) {
            if (last[c] == -1) continue;

            int l = first[c], r = last[c];
            bool valid = true;

            for (int i = l; i <= r; ++i) {
                int x = s[i] - 'a';

                if (first[x] < l) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }

                r = max(r, last[x]);
            }

            if (valid && (l != 0 || r != n - 1))
                intervals.push_back({r, l});
        }

        sort(intervals.begin(), intervals.end());

        int end = -1;

        for (auto &p : intervals) {
            if (p.second > end) {
                end = p.first;
                if (--k == 0) return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3459"></a>
## 3459. Length of Longest V-Shaped Diagonal Segment

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/length-of-longest-v-shaped-diagonal-segment/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2167654194/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-09 20:13 UTC

```cpp

class Solution {
    int n, m;
    int dr[4] = {1, 1, -1, -1};
    int dc[4] = {1, -1, -1, 1};
    vector<int> dp;

    int dfs(vector<vector<int>>& grid, int r, int c,
            int d, int turn) {
        int id = ((r * m + c) * 4 + d) * 2 + turn;

        if (dp[id] != -1)
            return dp[id];

        int next = grid[r][c] == 1 ? 2 : 2 - grid[r][c];
        int ans = 1;

        int x = r + dr[d], y = c + dc[d];

        if (x >= 0 && x < n && y >= 0 && y < m &&
            grid[x][y] == next)
            ans = max(ans, 1 + dfs(grid, x, y, d, turn));

        if (turn) {
            int nd = (d + 1) % 4;
            x = r + dr[nd];
            y = c + dc[nd];

            if (x >= 0 && x < n && y >= 0 && y < m &&
                grid[x][y] == next)
                ans = max(ans, 1 + dfs(grid, x, y, nd, 0));
        }

        return dp[id] = ans;
    }

public:
    int lenOfVDiagonal(vector<vector<int>>& grid) {
        n = grid.size();
        m = grid[0].size();

        dp.assign(n * m * 8, -1);
        int ans = 0;

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
                if (grid[i][j] != 1) continue;

                for (int d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
                    ans = max(ans, dfs(grid, i, j, d, 1));
            }
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3980"></a>
## 3980. Minimum Operations to Transform Binary String

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-operations-to-transform-binary-string/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163828147/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-06 05:04 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minOperations(string s1, string s2) {
        const int INF = 1e9;
        int n = s1.size();

        int dp0 = 0;
        int dp1 = INF;

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            int next0 = INF;
            int next1 = INF;

            // Current bit is original s1[i]
            if (dp0 < INF) {
                int cur = s1[i] - '0';
                int tar = s2[i] - '0';

                // Don't use pair operation
                if (cur == tar)
                    next0 = min(next0, dp0);
                else if (cur == 0 && tar == 1)
                    next0 = min(next0, dp0 + 1);

                // Use 11 -> 00 on i, i+1
                if (i + 1 < n) {
                    int cost = 1;

                    if (cur == 0) ++cost;
                    if (s1[i + 1] == '0') ++cost;
                    if (tar == 1) ++cost;

                    next1 = min(next1, dp0 + cost);
                }
            }

            // Current bit was already changed to 0
            // by pair operation from previous position
            if (dp1 < INF) {
                int cur = 0;
                int tar = s2[i] - '0';

                // Don't use pair operation
                if (cur == tar)
                    next0 = min(next0, dp1);
                else // 0 -> 1
                    next0 = min(next0, dp1 + 1);

                // Use pair operation on i, i+1
                if (i + 1 < n) {
                    int cost = 1;

                    // cur is 0, so first make it 1
                    ++cost;

                    if (s1[i + 1] == '0')
                        ++cost;

                    if (tar == 1)
                        ++cost;

                    next1 = min(next1, dp1 + cost);
                }
            }

            dp0 = next0;
            dp1 = next1;
        }

        return dp0 == INF ? -1 : dp0;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3981"></a>
## 3981. Count Distinct Ways to Form Target from Two Strings

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/count-distinct-ways-to-form-target-from-two-strings/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163815845/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-06 04:53 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int interleaveCharacters(string word1, string word2, string target) {
        const int MOD = 1000000007;

        int n = word1.size();
        int m = word2.size();

        vector<vector<long long> > dp(n + 1,
                                      vector<long long>(m + 1, 0));

        dp[0][0] = 1;

        for (char c : target) {

            vector<vector<long long> > col(n + 1,
                                           vector<long long>(m + 1, 0));

            vector<vector<long long> > row(n + 1,
                                           vector<long long>(m + 1, 0));

            // Column prefix
            for (int j = 0; j <= m; ++j) {
                for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
                    col[i][j] =
                        (col[i - 1][j] + dp[i - 1][j]) % MOD;
                }
            }

            // Row prefix
            for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i) {
                for (int j = 1; j <= m; ++j) {
                    row[i][j] =
                        (row[i][j - 1] + dp[i][j - 1]) % MOD;
                }
            }

            vector<vector<long long> > ndp(n + 1,
                                           vector<long long>(m + 1, 0));

            for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i) {
                for (int j = 0; j <= m; ++j) {

                    // Take current character from word1
                    if (i > 0 && word1[i - 1] == c)
                        ndp[i][j] += col[i][j];

                    // Take current character from word2
                    if (j > 0 && word2[j - 1] == c)
                        ndp[i][j] += row[i][j];

                    ndp[i][j] %= MOD;
                }
            }

            dp.swap(ndp);
        }

        long long ans = 0;

        // Must use at least one character from both strings
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
            for (int j = 1; j <= m; ++j) {
                ans = (ans + dp[i][j]) % MOD;
            }
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3984"></a>
## 3984. Divisible Game

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/divisible-game/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163804968/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-06 04:42 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int divisibleGame(vector<int>& nums) {
        const long long MOD = 1000000007;
        set<int> primes;

        // Collect unique prime factors
        for (int x : nums) {
            int n = x;

            for (int p = 2; 1LL * p * p <= n; ++p) {
                if (n % p == 0) {
                    primes.insert(p);

                    while (n % p == 0)
                        n /= p;
                }
            }

            if (n > 1)
                primes.insert(n);
        }

        // Important for nums containing only 1
        if (primes.empty())
            primes.insert(2);

        long long bestScore = LLONG_MIN;
        int bestK = 2;

        for (int k : primes) {
            long long curr = 0;
            long long best = LLONG_MIN;

            // Kadane
            for (int x : nums) {
                long long val = (x % k == 0) ? x : -x;

                curr += val;
                best = max(best, curr);

                if (curr < 0)
                    curr = 0;
            }

            // set is ascending, so first k for best score
            // automatically gives smallest k
            if (best > bestScore) {
                bestScore = best;
                bestK = k;
            }
        }

        return ((bestScore % MOD + MOD) % MOD * bestK) % MOD;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3989"></a>
## 3989. Maximum Consistent Columns in a Grid

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-consistent-columns-in-a-grid/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163794753/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-06 04:32 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int maxConsistentColumns(vector<vector<int>>& grid, int limit) {
          int m = grid.size(), n = grid[0].size();
        vector<int> dp(n, 1);
        int ans = 1;

        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            for (int k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                bool ok = true;

                for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
                    if (abs(grid[i][j] - grid[i][k]) > limit) {
                        ok = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (ok)
                    dp[j] = max(dp[j], dp[k] + 1);
            }

            ans = max(ans, dp[j]);
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-3995"></a>
## 3995. Minimum Cost to Convert String III

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-cost-to-convert-string-iii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163779815/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-06 04:16 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minCost(string source, string target,
                vector<vector<string>>& rules,
                vector<int>& costs) {

        int n = source.size(), m = rules.size();
        const long long INF = 1e18;

        vector<long long> dp(n + 1, INF);
        dp[0] = 0;

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            if (dp[i] == INF) continue;

            // No operation needed at this position
            if (source[i] == target[i])
                dp[i + 1] = min(dp[i + 1], dp[i]);

            // Try every rule starting at i
            for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
                string &p = rules[j][0];
                string &r = rules[j][1];

                int len = p.size();
                if (i + len > n) continue;

                bool ok = true;
                int cost = costs[j];

                for (int k = 0; k < len; ++k) {

                    // Replacement must exactly match target
                    if (r[k] != target[i + k]) {
                        ok = false;
                        break;
                    }

                    // Pattern must match source
                    if (p[k] == '*')
                        ++cost;
                    else if (p[k] != source[i + k]) {
                        ok = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (ok)
                    dp[i + len] =
                        min(dp[i + len],
                            dp[i] + cost);
            }
        }

        return dp[n] == INF ? -1 : (int)dp[n];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-4009"></a>
## 4009. Minimum Possible Maximum Waiting Time

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-possible-maximum-waiting-time/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163651869/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-05 23:57 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minMaxWaitingTime(vector<int>& d, vector<int>& fuel) {
        vector<int> telmorvian = d;

        // state = {fuel0, fuel1, busy0, busy1, maxWait}
        vector<array<int,5> > cur;
        cur.push_back({fuel[0], fuel[1], 0, 0, 0});

        int ans = -1;

        for (int x : d) {
            map<array<int,4>, int> best;

            for (auto s : cur) {
                for (int p = 0; p < 2; ++p) {
                    int f0 = s[0], f1 = s[1];
                    int b0 = s[2], b1 = s[3];
                    int w  = s[4];

                    int &f = (p == 0 ? f0 : f1);
                    int &b = (p == 0 ? b0 : b1);

                    if (f < x) continue;

                    int wait = b;
                    f -= x;

                    if (p == 0) {
                        b0 = x;
                        b1 = max(0, b1 - wait);
                    } else {
                        b1 = x;
                        b0 = max(0, b0 - wait);
                    }

                    array<int,4> key = {f0, f1, b0, b1};
                    int nw = max(w, wait);

                    if (!best.count(key) || nw < best[key])
                        best[key] = nw;
                }
            }

            if (best.empty()) break;

            cur.clear();
            ans = INT_MAX;

            for (auto &e : best) {
                cur.push_back({
                    e.first[0], e.first[1],
                    e.first[2], e.first[3],
                    e.second
                });

                ans = min(ans, e.second);
            }
        }

        return ans;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-4016"></a>
## 4016. Maximum Area of Two Non-Overlapping Square Submatrices

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-area-of-two-non-overlapping-square-submatrices/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163650018/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-05 23:50 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
private:
    int m, n;
    vector<vector<int> > prefix;

    // Sum of rectangle:
    // [r1, c1] -> [r2, c2], inclusive
    int getSum(int r1, int c1, int r2, int c2) {
        return prefix[r2 + 1][c2 + 1]
             - prefix[r1][c2 + 1]
             - prefix[r2 + 1][c1]
             + prefix[r1][c1];
    }

    bool possible(int k) {
        int minRow = m;
        int maxRow = -1;

        int minCol = n;
        int maxCol = -1;

        int count = 0;

        for (int r = 0; r + k <= m; ++r) {
            for (int c = 0; c + k <= n; ++c) {

                // Check if entire k x k square is 1.
                if (getSum(r, c,
                           r + k - 1,
                           c + k - 1) == k * k) {

                    ++count;

                    minRow = min(minRow, r);
                    maxRow = max(maxRow, r);

                    minCol = min(minCol, c);
                    maxCol = max(maxCol, c);
                }
            }
        }

        if (count < 2)
            return false;

        // Two squares separated vertically
        if (maxRow - minRow >= k)
            return true;

        // Two squares separated horizontally
        if (maxCol - minCol >= k)
            return true;

        return false;
    }

public:
    int maxArea(vector<vector<int> >& mat) {
        m = mat.size();
        n = mat[0].size();

        // --------------------------------
        // Build 2D prefix sum
        // --------------------------------
        prefix.assign(m + 1,
                      vector<int>(n + 1, 0));

        for (int r = 0; r < m; ++r) {
            for (int c = 0; c < n; ++c) {

                prefix[r + 1][c + 1] =
                    mat[r][c]
                    + prefix[r][c + 1]
                    + prefix[r + 1][c]
                    - prefix[r][c];
            }
        }

        // --------------------------------
        // Binary search side length
        // --------------------------------
        int low = 1;
        int high = min(m, n);

        int best = 0;

        while (low <= high) {

            int mid = low + (high - low) / 2;

            if (possible(mid)) {
                best = mid;
                low = mid + 1;
            }
            else {
                high = mid - 1;
            }
        }

        return best * best;
    }
};
```
