<a id="problem-4023"></a>
## 4023. Elevator Requests II

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/elevator-requests-ii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163649209/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-05 23:47 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    long long elevatorRequests(int n, int start,
                               vector<int>& requests) {
        // Remove start because its request is fulfilled at time 0.
        vector<long long> a;

        for (int x : requests) {
            if (x != start)
                a.push_back(x);
        }

        int m = (int)a.size();

        // All requests already fulfilled.
        if (m == 0)
            return 0;

        // Insert starting floor.
        a.push_back(start);
        sort(a.begin(), a.end());

        int sz = m + 1;

        int s = lower_bound(a.begin(), a.end(),
                            (long long)start) - a.begin();

        const long long INF = LLONG_MAX / 4;

        // dp[l][r][0] -> currently at a[l]
        // dp[l][r][1] -> currently at a[r]
        vector<vector<array<long long, 2> > > dp(
            sz,
            vector<array<long long, 2> >(
                sz, array<long long, 2>{INF, INF}
            )
        );

        dp[s][s][0] = 0;
        dp[s][s][1] = 0;

        // Current interval length.
        for (int len = 1; len <= sz; ++len) {

            // Number of actual requests already served.
            int served = len - 1;

            // Requests that are still waiting.
            int remaining = m - served;

            for (int l = 0; l + len - 1 < sz; ++l) {

                int r = l + len - 1;

                // Interval must contain start.
                if (l > s || r < s)
                    continue;

                // -----------------------------------
                // Expand LEFT: include a[l-1]
                // -----------------------------------
                if (l > 0) {

                    // Currently at left endpoint.
                    if (dp[l][r][0] != INF) {

                        long long dist =
                            a[l] - a[l - 1];

                        dp[l - 1][r][0] =
                            min(dp[l - 1][r][0],
                                dp[l][r][0]
                                + dist * remaining);
                    }

                    // Currently at right endpoint.
                    if (dp[l][r][1] != INF) {

                        long long dist =
                            a[r] - a[l - 1];

                        dp[l - 1][r][0] =
                            min(dp[l - 1][r][0],
                                dp[l][r][1]
                                + dist * remaining);
                    }
                }

                // -----------------------------------
                // Expand RIGHT: include a[r+1]
                // -----------------------------------
                if (r + 1 < sz) {

                    // Currently at left endpoint.
                    if (dp[l][r][0] != INF) {

                        long long dist =
                            a[r + 1] - a[l];

                        dp[l][r + 1][1] =
                            min(dp[l][r + 1][1],
                                dp[l][r][0]
                                + dist * remaining);
                    }

                    // Currently at right endpoint.
                    if (dp[l][r][1] != INF) {

                        long long dist =
                            a[r + 1] - a[r];

                        dp[l][r + 1][1] =
                            min(dp[l][r + 1][1],
                                dp[l][r][1]
                                + dist * remaining);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return min(dp[0][sz - 1][0],
                   dp[0][sz - 1][1]);
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-4027"></a>
## 4027. Elevator Requests III

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/elevator-requests-iii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163648642/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-05 23:45 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    long long elevatorRequests(int n, int start,
                               vector<vector<int>>& requests) {
        
        int m = requests.size();
        int totalMasks = 1 << m;

        const long long INF = (1LL << 60);

        // dp[mask][i] =
        // minimum time after fulfilling all requests in mask,
        // with request i fulfilled last.
        vector<vector<long long>> dp(
            totalMasks,
            vector<long long>(m, INF)
        );

        // ----------------------------------
        // Initialize first request
        // ----------------------------------
        for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {

            long long travel =
                llabs((long long)start - requests[i][1]);

            dp[1 << i][i] =
                max(travel,
                    (long long)requests[i][0]);
        }

        // ----------------------------------
        // Bitmask DP
        // ----------------------------------
        for (int mask = 1; mask < totalMasks; ++mask) {

            for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {

                // i must be the last fulfilled request
                if (!(mask & (1 << i)))
                    continue;

                if (dp[mask][i] == INF)
                    continue;

                // Choose next request j
                for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {

                    if (mask & (1 << j))
                        continue;

                    long long distance =
                        llabs((long long)requests[i][1]
                              - requests[j][1]);

                    long long reachTime =
                        dp[mask][i] + distance;

                    // If we arrive before request j appears,
                    // wait until its arrival time.
                    long long newTime =
                        max(reachTime,
                            (long long)requests[j][0]);

                    int newMask =
                        mask | (1 << j);

                    dp[newMask][j] =
                        min(dp[newMask][j],
                            newTime);
                }
            }
        }

        // ----------------------------------
        // Answer
        // ----------------------------------
        int fullMask = totalMasks - 1;

        long long answer = INF;

        for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
            answer = min(answer,
                         dp[fullMask][i]);
        }

        return answer;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-4040"></a>
## 4040. Minimum Operations to Form Subset Sum I

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-operations-to-form-subset-sum-i/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163647519/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-05 23:41 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minOperations(vector<int>& nums, int sum) {
        const int INF = 1e9;

        // dp[s] = min operations to form sum s
        vector<int> dp(sum + 1, INF);
        dp[0] = 0;

        for (int x : nums) {

            // (reachable value, operation cost)
            vector<pair<int, int> > options;

            // -----------------------------
            // Option 1: Original value
            // -----------------------------
            if (x <= sum) {
                options.push_back({x, 0});
            }

            // -----------------------------
            // Option 2: Repeated division
            // x -> x/2 -> x/4 -> ...
            // -----------------------------
            int value = x;
            int cost = 0;

            while (value > 1) {
                value /= 2;
                ++cost;

                options.push_back({value, cost});
            }

            // -----------------------------
            // Option 3: Repeated multiplication
            // x -> 2x -> 4x -> ...
            // -----------------------------
            long long v = 2LL * x;
            cost = 1;

            while (v <= sum) {
                options.push_back({(int)v, cost});

                v *= 2;
                ++cost;
            }

            // -----------------------------
            // 0/1 Knapsack
            // -----------------------------
            vector<int> next = dp;

            for (int s = 0; s <= sum; ++s) {

                if (dp[s] == INF)
                    continue;

                for (int j = 0; j < (int)options.size(); ++j) {

                    int val = options[j].first;
                    int op  = options[j].second;

                    if (s + val <= sum) {
                        next[s + val] =
                            min(next[s + val],
                                dp[s] + op);
                    }
                }
            }

            dp.swap(next);

            // Can't improve beyond zero operations
            if (dp[sum] == 0)
                return 0;
        }

        return dp[sum] == INF ? -1 : dp[sum];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-4041"></a>
## 4041. Minimum Operations to Form Subset Sum II

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-operations-to-form-subset-sum-ii/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163645696/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-05 23:34 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minOperations(vector<int>& nums, int target) {
        const int INF = 1e9;

        // dp[s] = minimum operations to form sum s
        vector<int> dp(target + 1, INF);
        dp[0] = 0;

        for (int x : nums) {

            // Generate only reachable values.
            // best[v] = minimum cost to transform x -> v
            vector<pair<int, int> > options;

            // Temporarily keep best cost.
            unordered_map<int, int> best;

            int cur = x;
            int divOps = 0;

            // x -> x/2 -> x/4 -> ...
            while (cur > 0) {

                long long value = cur;
                int mulOps = 0;

                // Then multiply by 2 repeatedly.
                while (value <= target) {

                    int v = (int)value;
                    int cost = divOps + mulOps;

                    auto it = best.find(v);

                    if (it == best.end() || cost < it->second)
                        best[v] = cost;

                    value *= 2;
                    ++mulOps;
                }

                cur /= 2;
                ++divOps;
            }

            // Convert to compact list.
            for (auto &p : best) {
                options.push_back({p.first, p.second});
            }

            // 0/1 knapsack
            vector<int> next = dp;

            for (int s = 0; s <= target; ++s) {

                if (dp[s] == INF)
                    continue;

                for (int j = 0; j < (int)options.size(); ++j) {

                    int value = options[j].first;
                    int cost  = options[j].second;

                    if (s + value <= target) {
                        next[s + value] =
                            min(next[s + value],
                                dp[s] + cost);
                    }
                }
            }

            dp.swap(next);

            // Can't do better than 0 operations.
            if (dp[target] == 0)
                return 0;
        }

        return dp[target] == INF ? -1 : dp[target];
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-4050"></a>
## 4050. Minimum Days to Score Exactly N Points

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/minimum-days-to-score-exactly-n-points/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163644881/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-05 23:31 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    int minDays(int n) {
        const int INF = 1e9;

        vector<int> dp(n + 1, INF);
        dp[0] = 0;

        // Generate all possible streak lengths.
        for (int k = 1; ; ++k) {
            int points = k * (k + 1) / 2;

            if (points > n)
                break;

            int cost = k + 1;

            // Unbounded knapsack:
            // same streak length can be used multiple times.
            for (int score = points; score <= n; ++score) {
                dp[score] = min(
                    dp[score],
                    dp[score - points] + cost
                );
            }
        }

        // Last streak doesn't need a skip after it.
        return dp[n] - 1;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-4058"></a>
## 4058. Maximum Pulse Value After One Subarray Rotation

**Difficulty:** Medium  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximum-pulse-value-after-one-subarray-rotation/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163642024/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-05 23:19 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    long long maxValue(vector<int>& nums) {
        int n = nums.size();

        // Alternating prefix sum
        vector<long long> P(n + 1, 0);

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            if (i % 2 == 0)
                P[i + 1] = P[i] + nums[i];
            else
                P[i + 1] = P[i] - nums[i];
        }

        long long original = P[n];
        long long maxGain = 0;

        const long long NEG = LLONG_MIN / 4;

        // For same parity:
        // we need max P[l+1]
        long long maxSame[2] = {NEG, NEG};

        // For different parity:
        // we need max P[l]
        long long maxDiff[2] = {NEG, NEG};

        // Scan r from left to right.
        for (int r = 1; r < n; ++r) {

            // Before processing r, add l = r-1.
            // This guarantees l < r.
            int l = r - 1;

            maxSame[l % 2] =
                max(maxSame[l % 2], P[l + 1]);

            maxDiff[l % 2] =
                max(maxDiff[l % 2], P[l]);

            // ---------------------------------
            // Case 1: l and r same parity
            // gain = 2 * (P[l+1] - P[r+1])
            // ---------------------------------
            if (maxSame[r % 2] != NEG) {
                long long gain =
                    2LL * (maxSame[r % 2] - P[r + 1]);

                maxGain = max(maxGain, gain);
            }

            // ---------------------------------
            // Case 2: l and r different parity
            // gain = 2 * (P[l] - P[r+1])
            // ---------------------------------
            int opposite = 1 - (r % 2);

            if (maxDiff[opposite] != NEG) {
                long long gain =
                    2LL * (maxDiff[opposite] - P[r + 1]);

                maxGain = max(maxGain, gain);
            }
        }

        return original + maxGain;
    }
};
```

<a id="problem-4068"></a>
## 4068. Maximize Meeting Earnings with Idle Gaps

**Difficulty:** Hard  
**Problem:** https://leetcode.com/problems/maximize-meeting-earnings-with-idle-gaps/  
**Submission:** https://leetcode.com/submissions/detail/2163636693/  
**Language:** cpp  
**Submitted:** 2026-10-05 22:59 UTC

```cpp
class Solution {
public:
    long long maxEarnings(vector<vector<int>>& meetings) {

        // Sort meetings by end time
        sort(meetings.begin(), meetings.end(),
             [](const vector<int>& a, const vector<int>& b) {
                 return a[1] < b[1];
             });

        int n = meetings.size();

        vector<long long> ends(n);

        // best[i] =
        // maximum (dp[j] - end[j]) among meetings [0 ... i-1]
        //
        // best has size n+1 so best[0] means no previous meeting.
        vector<long long> best(n + 1, LLONG_MIN);

        long long answer = 0;

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

            long long start   = meetings[i][0];
            long long end     = meetings[i][1];
            long long revenue = meetings[i][2];

            ends[i] = end;

            // Case 1:
            // This is the first selected meeting.
            long long dp = revenue;

            // Number of previous meetings whose end <= start.
            //
            // upper_bound(start) gives first end > start.
            int pos = upper_bound(
                          ends.begin(),
                          ends.begin() + i,
                          start
                      ) - ends.begin();

            // Case 2:
            // Connect this meeting to a previous compatible meeting.
            if (pos > 0) {
                dp = max(dp,
                         revenue + start + best[pos]);
            }

            // Store prefix maximum of:
            // dp[j] - end[j]
            best[i + 1] = max(best[i],
                              dp - end);

            answer = max(answer, dp);
        }

        return answer;
    }
};
```
